# S8.3 — Enclosed courtyard and footprints with holes Implementation Plan

> **Status:** Done (controller, D-084; pending the owner's reading) · **Date:** 2026-10-02 · **Roadmap:** [S8.3](2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md#s8--typologies-s81s87-d-083-d-084-d-098) · **Decisions:** D-009, D-026, D-041, D-061, D-064, D-065, D-076, D-079, D-082, D-083, D-084, D-087, D-088, D-090, D-091; [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md), [ADR-002](../decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md) (Tasks 1 and 9), [ADR-008](../decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md) (Tasks 3 and 9), [ADR-013](../decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) (Tasks 4 and 9)
>
> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement S8.3 of D-084: footprints with holes in every pipeline step, and the plan generator of the last *High-Density Housing* family, `HDHM-TYP-005` (*Enclosed Court*, four wings of stair bays around one court closed on all four sides), as ADR-006 specifies it, with its Grasshopper component and icon. The court is a hole of the footprint, outside the building: its edges are outdoor walls with windows that face into it, and it has no zone, floor, ceiling, ground slab, or roof. The surface builder now also requires every footprint edge, court edges included, to be covered by outdoor zone edges, so a zone filling a court is an error (`UnmatchedEdge`); *Perimeter Core* zones the court façades in their own zones `P-Court-<Bin>` and leaves a core that is a ring around the court, with its width precondition (D-087) applying to every wing between the outer and a court façade. *No Simplification*, *Semantic Merge*, *Single Zone per Floor*, the four floor aggregators, and validation already handle a hole; tests on a ring footprint show it. The enclosed court validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plan and rotated and moved by two placements. The headless Rhino check of the reference found that `RhinoGeometry` built every zone solid inward-oriented and every floor facing up, on every plan released up to `v0.8.1`; S8.3 applies the orientation rule of D-091 (zone solids outward, every surface and window facing out of its zone). Every existing plan, floor, building, and snapshot stays unchanged. S8.3 ends with version 0.8.2 and the local tag `v0.8.2`; nothing is pushed in this run (D-084).

**Architecture:** `LayoutSurfaceBuilder` gains a check after edge classification: the outdoor segments on each edge of every footprint ring must add up to the edge's length (`ToleranceSettings.AreaEquals`, as façade coverage is compared). `PerimeterCore` offsets every ring of the footprint with the ADR-008 mitre formula (the outer ring counter-clockwise and the holes clockwise, so the floor always lies on the ring's left), checks each offset ring, and, with holes, checks that clipping the offset holes from the offset outer ring gives one polygon of the core's area; the trapezoids of the hole edges are united per bin into `P-Court-<Bin>` zones, listed after the outer zones and before `CORE`. The new family is a folder `Lod.Generators/EnclosedCourt/` with a parameter record (a static `Default` holding the ADR-006 defaults), a preset record with one slot per space type, and a generator whose four wings are laid out by the S8.2 `StairBayLayout.Wing`, `Fit`, and `FitErrors`; the footprint is `Polygon2.Create(outer, new[] { court })`. In `Lod.Grasshopper` one component derives from the S8.1 `TypologyGeneratorComponent`; `scripts/make_icons.py` draws its icon in the Generate green; `RhinoGeometry` builds planar faces unreversed with the facing they need (rings reversed for a face facing down), extrudes zone solids from the downward floor, and guards both with `Outward` and `Facing`.

**Tech Stack:** C# 12 (`LangVersion` 12.0); .NET SDK ≥ 8 (`global.json` pins 8.0.100 with `rollForward: latestMajor`); `netstandard2.0` core libraries; Clipper2 2.0.0; RhinoCommon and Grasshopper 8.19.25132.1001 (plugin targets `net7.0`); xUnit 2.9.3, xunit.runner.visualstudio 3.1.5, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.14.1 (tests target `net8.0`); Windows PowerShell 5.1 for `scripts/verify.ps1`; Python 3.10 or later with Pillow for `scripts/make_icons.py` (the icons in this plan were drawn with Pillow 12.3.0).

## Global Constraints

- **Gate:** D-090 and D-091 are recorded on `main` (`87bd272`), and this plan is committed to `main` together with the roadmap's S8.3 stage edits (see [Roadmap edits](#roadmap-edits)) before Task 1 starts. ADR-006 and ADR-011, which specify the enclosed court and the minimum wall and window geometry, were accepted in S8.1 (roadmap S8 gate, D-084); S8.3 changes no record or default of ADR-006. S8.3 depends on S8.2, which is tagged `v0.8.1` locally. Before Task 1 the working tree is clean (`git status --short` prints nothing).
- Rhino 8 (8.19 or later) only (D-003); stages exchange Grasshopper objects, not JSON (D-017).
- `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` have no RhinoCommon or Grasshopper reference; their tests run with plain `dotnet test`.
- Nullable reference types on; warnings are errors; `.editorconfig` rules are enforced in the build; every public member of `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` has XML docs (CS1591 is an error).
- Generation is deterministic; outputs have a stable order: the enclosed court lists its south and north wings (west to east), then its west and east wings (south to north), each wing stairs first, then dwellings, numbered along the wing's axis; *Perimeter Core* lists the outer zones North, East, South, West, then the court zones North, East, South, West, then `CORE`.
- Numerical tolerances come only from `ToleranceSettings` (ADR-004); S8.3 adds none and changes none. The boundary coverage of Task 1 compares lengths with the existing relative tolerance (`AreaEquals`), as façade coverage does (D-041); the `1e-6` margins in the test helpers that find court walls are test geometry, not tolerances of the code. `ToleranceSettings.cs` is not touched. The minimum wall and window geometry stays in `GeometryLimits` (ADR-011), unchanged.
- **Existing outputs do not change.** The fifteen existing snapshots (`linear-plan-canonical`, `stack-two-storeys`, the three `simplifier-*`, the three `aggregator-*`, the four S8.1 canonical snapshots `stair-bay-bar-canonical`, `stair-pair-canonical`, `gallery-bar-canonical`, `terrace-row-canonical`, and the three S8.2 canonical snapshots `point-plate-canonical`, `winged-band-canonical`, `open-court-canonical`) stay byte-identical, and every existing test keeps passing, except the one test that pinned the behaviour S8.3 replaces: `SimplifierTests.FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet` is removed in Task 3, because *Perimeter Core* now zones footprints with holes (D-084). The check of Task 1 rejects only layouts that leave a footprint edge uncovered; no existing plan does.
- **Never weaken a check (AGENTS.md rules 11, 12).** Task 1 adds a check; Task 3 keeps every check of *Perimeter Core* and adds one for holes. Each code change in `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` starts from a test that fails the way the task says; Tasks 2, 4, 5, 6, and 8 add tests that pass at once, because the steps they cover already handle holes (each says so and says to stop if a case fails). No tolerance is widened, no validation check is relaxed, and no other test is weakened or removed.
- **Report, never repair (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6).** A length that is not positive and finite, and stairs that leave no dwelling width in a wing, are the error `InvalidParameter`, every one reported at once. A footprint that *Perimeter Core* cannot zone, including a wing between the outer and a court façade that is not wider than twice the depth, fails with `PlanTooNarrow`; it is never repaired. The generator does not impose *Perimeter Core*'s precondition (D-087): it belongs to that simplifier. Zones about 1 m wide on rotated plans remain the known limit of D-090; S8.3 does not change it.
- **Orientation only (D-091).** Task 13 changes the orientation of the Breps `RhinoGeometry` creates (zone solids, walls, floors, ceilings, windows, and the merged preview volumes), never their geometry. It applies to every plan generator, not only the enclosed court, and repairs output released up to `v0.8.1`. The *Convert2BEM* output layout is unchanged.
- **Typed records per family** (AGENTS.md): the enclosed court has its own parameter and preset records; there is no catch-all parameter class and no dictionary of parameters. Its space types are the existing `DwellingUnit` and `Stair`, so the existing default preset components serve it.
- **Source contract (D-082).** No book text, page images, or copied drawings enter BEMGen; ADR-006 and the precedent page paraphrase the family with its stable ID. The source repository is not opened or modified while this plan is executed; every text the tasks need is given here.
- **No backward compatibility (D-064).** One component is added with a new GUID: *Enclosed Court* `b9765cd0-a62b-483d-9470-86d5fda287e8`. No existing component, input, or GUID changes; the outputs of existing components keep their layout, and only the orientation of the Breps of *Convert2BEM* and of the previews changes (D-091).
- Grasshopper components are thin adaptors (AGENTS.md): every rule lives in `Lod.Core` or `Lod.Generators`; the component reads inputs, calls the generator, and reports its diagnostics.
- Research equivalence rules are not changed (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7): a court façade counts in the glazing of the orientation it faces, as every façade does. The documents that state the rules S8.3 extends (ADR-002 for the boundary coverage, ADR-008 for the court perimeter zones, ADR-013 for the floor aggregators) are updated in Task 9, in the same slice as the code, once the enclosed court they cite exists; the slice is merged only after Task 9.
- No simulation inside the pipeline (D-016). No CI (D-005): `scripts/verify.ps1` passes locally before every merge to `main`.
- Commits: `type(scope): summary` in lower case, no agent, model, or tool attribution and no `Co-Authored-By` trailers (D-001, D-002); author identity per D-050.
- **Pushes (D-084).** Nothing is pushed in this run: slices are rebased onto local `main`, and the stage is tagged `v0.8.2` locally. Task 18 (the push) is not performed in this run; it waits for the owner.
- `.gh` example definitions are made and saved by a person in Rhino 8; agents never write or check them (D-058). S8.3 adds none.
- S8.3 changes these earlier files: `LayoutSurfaceBuilder.cs`, `LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests.cs` (S2, last changed in S8.2), `Strategies.cs`, `SimplifierTests.cs` (S3, last changed in S8.2), `StairBayLayout.cs`, `TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1, last changed in S8.2), `RhinoGeometry.cs` (S2, last changed by `4b07283` after `v0.5.0`), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1, last changed in S8.2), and `Directory.Build.props` (S0, last changed in S8.2); and the documents ADR-002, ADR-008, ADR-013 (last changed in S8.2), ADR-006 (S8.1, last changed by `34deeaa` after `v0.8.1`), `docs/architecture/convert2bem.md` (S2, last changed by `4b07283`), `docs/architecture/pipeline.md`, `docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md`, `docs/research/program-presets.md`, and `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` (last changed in S8.2), and `docs/architecture/validation.md` (S3, last changed in S8.1).

## Slice A — `feature/footprints-with-holes`

The tasks of this slice make every pipeline step correct for a footprint with holes, first on a simple ring footprint (four wings around a square court, `RingPlan`) and then on the enclosed court generator, and record the rules in the ADRs (Task 9) before the slice is merged. Tasks 1 and 3 change code, each from a test that fails the way the task says; Tasks 2, 4, 5, and 6 add tests that pass at once, because the steps they cover already handle holes; if one of them fails, stop and report: a further pipeline change needs its own decision and test-first task.

### Task 1: Every footprint edge, court edges included, is covered by outdoor zone edges

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Core/Layout/LayoutSurfaceBuilder.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S8.2)
- Test: modify `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Layout/LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S8.2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LayoutSurfaceBuilder.Build(Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, double height) : Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>` (S2; `Classify` marks a segment on any footprint ring as outdoors, S8.2 `VertexGrid.IsOnSegment`), `Polygon2.Create(IEnumerable<Point2> outer, IEnumerable<IEnumerable<Point2>>? holes)` (holes stored clockwise), `Polygon2.Rings`, `ToleranceSettings.AreaEquals(double expected, double actual)` (relative), `PlanMotion(double rotationDegrees, double translationX, double translationY)` and `Polygon2.Transform(PlanMotion)` (S4.2), `Orientation.Bin`, `DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge` (S2).
- Produces: after classification, `LayoutSurfaceBuilder` checks every edge of every footprint ring: the outdoor segments lying on it must add up to its length within `AreaEquals`; otherwise the error `UnmatchedEdge` with the message `Plan boundary edge (x, y)-(x, y) is the outdoor edge of zones over {covered} m of its {length} m (a zone lies outside the footprint, such as in a court, or the zones leave a gap).` The surfaces of every valid layout are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 1: Create the branch**

```bash
git switch -c feature/footprints-with-holes main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — replace `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Layout/LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests.cs` (full file)**

Four new tests on a 30 m × 30 m footprint with a 10 m × 10 m court and four 10 m deep wings: `CourtEdgesBecomeOutdoorWallsFacingIntoTheCourt` (the four court walls belong to `S`, `N`, `W`, `E` and face north, south, east, and west, 10 m each; 480 m² of outdoor wall; 8 interzone walls), `AZoneWithAHoleHasOutdoorWallsOnEveryRing` (one zone equal to the footprint has 8 outdoor walls, four of them on the court facing every direction, and its floor is the ring), `AZoneFillingTheCourtIsAnError` (a fifth zone in the court, every edge shared with a wing, gives `UnmatchedEdge`), and `ARotatedRingKeepsItsCourtWalls` (rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m: 12 outdoor walls, 480 m²). Every other test is unchanged.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Layout;

public sealed class LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests
{
    private readonly LayoutSurfaceBuilder _builder = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneHasFourOutdoorWallsAndUnresolvedFloorAndCeiling()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] walls = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, walls.Length);
        Assert.All(walls, w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(30.0 * 3.0, walls.Sum(w => w.Area), 9);
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { HorizontalKind.Floor, HorizontalKind.Ceiling }, horizontal.Select(h => h.Kind));
        Assert.All(horizontal, h => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, h.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(3.0, horizontal[1].Elevation);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SharedEdgeBecomesAPairOfInterzoneWalls()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface ab = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "A" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
        WallSurface ba = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "B" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
        Assert.Equal("B", ab.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value);
        Assert.Equal("A", ba.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value);
        Assert.Equal(ab.Start, ba.End);
        Assert.Equal(ab.End, ba.Start);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EdgesAreSplitWhereAnotherZoneTouchesThem()
    {
        // A long zone above two short ones: its south edge is shared with both.
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 10),
            Zone("TOP", 0, 5, 20, 10),
            Zone("L", 0, 0, 12, 5),
            Zone("R", 12, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] shared = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "TOP" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(2, shared.Length);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 12.0, 8.0 }.OrderBy(x => x), shared.Select(w => w.Length).OrderBy(x => x));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CollinearPiecesOfOneEdgeMergeIntoOneWall()
    {
        // The plan boundary has an extra vertex halfway along the north edge, which splits the zone's edge; the pieces merge back.
        Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(20, 0), new Point2(20, 5), new Point2(10, 5), new Point2(0, 5) }).Value;

        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(footprint, Zone("A", 0, 0, 20, 5)).Value;

        WallSurface[] walls = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, walls.Length);
        Assert.Contains(walls, w => w.Start == new Point2(20, 5) && w.End == new Point2(0, 5));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GapsAreErrors()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 9, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OverlapsAreErrors()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 0, 0, 10, 5));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.OverlappingZones);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WindowsAreSizedByEachZonesRatio()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio(
            Build(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 5), Zone("A", 0, 0, 10, 5), Zone("B", 10, 0, 20, 5)).Value,
            id => id.Value == "A" ? 0.5 : 0.1,
            GeometryLimits.Default,
            ToleranceSettings.Default).Value;

        WallSurface[] outdoor = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Zone.Value == "A" ? 0.5 : 0.1) * w.Area, w.GlazedArea, 9));
        Assert.All(surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone), w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RotatedTJunctionsOnTheBoundaryAndBetweenZonesAreRecognised()
    {
        // A pinwheel of four 8 m deep zones around a 6 m core, rotated by 131.4° and moved: the zone corners at (14, 0), (22, 14), (8, 22), and
        // (0, 8) lie on the boundary between its corners, and snapped to the distance grid the one at (8, 22) lies 1.10 grid units off the snapped
        // boundary edge although it is on the exact edge; the edge test measures the original points against the distance tolerance.
        var motion = new PlanMotion(131.4, 15.08, -153.9);
        LayoutZone Moved(string id, double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1) => new(new ZoneId(id), Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, y0, x1, y1).Transform(motion));

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = _builder.Build(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 22, 22).Transform(motion),
            new[] { Moved("ST", 8, 8, 14, 14), Moved("U1", 0, 0, 14, 8), Moved("U2", 14, 0, 22, 14), Moved("U3", 8, 14, 22, 22), Moved("U4", 0, 8, 8, 22) },
            3.0);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        WallSurface[] walls = result.Value.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(8, walls.Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
        Assert.Equal(22.0 * 4 * 3.0, walls.Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).Sum(w => w.Area), 6);
        Assert.Equal(16, walls.Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CourtEdgesBecomeOutdoorWallsFacingIntoTheCourt()
    {
        // Four 10 m deep wings around a 10 m × 10 m court: the court's edges lie on the footprint's hole and face into the court.
        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(Ring(), RingWings()).Value;

        WallSurface[] court = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w)).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("S", OrientationBin.North), ("N", OrientationBin.South), ("W", OrientationBin.East), ("E", OrientationBin.West) }, court.Select(w => (w.Zone.Value, Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Equal(10.0, w.Length, 12));
        Assert.Equal((120.0 + 40.0) * 3.0, surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).Sum(w => w.Area), 9);
        Assert.Equal(8, surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AZoneWithAHoleHasOutdoorWallsOnEveryRing()
    {
        Polygon2 ring = Ring();

        IReadOnlyList<Surface> surfaces = Build(ring, new LayoutZone(new ZoneId("A"), ring)).Value;

        WallSurface[] walls = surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(8, walls.Length);
        Assert.All(walls, w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }.OrderBy(b => b),
            walls.Where(OnCourt).Select(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth)).OrderBy(b => b));
        Assert.Equal(ring, Assert.IsType<HorizontalSurface>(surfaces.Single(s => s.Id.Value == "A/F")).Polygon);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AZoneFillingTheCourtIsAnError()
    {
        // Every edge of a zone in the court is shared with a wing, so only the footprint's hole edges, which no zone covers outdoors, reveal it.
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = Build(Ring(), RingWings().Append(Zone("C", 10, 10, 20, 20)).ToArray());

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ARotatedRingKeepsItsCourtWalls()
    {
        var motion = new PlanMotion(131.4, 15.08, -153.9);

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> result = _builder.Build(
            Ring().Transform(motion),
            RingWings().Select(z => z with { Footprint = z.Footprint.Transform(motion) }).ToArray(),
            3.0);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        WallSurface[] outdoor = result.Value.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(12, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.Equal((120.0 + 40.0) * 3.0, outdoor.Sum(w => w.Area), 6);
    }

    /// <summary>A 30 m × 30 m footprint with a 10 m × 10 m court in its middle.</summary>
    private static Polygon2 Ring() =>
        Polygon2.Create(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 30, 30).Outer, new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 10, 20, 20).Outer }).Value;

    /// <summary>The four wings of <see cref="Ring"/>: south and north full width, west and east between them.</summary>
    private static LayoutZone[] RingWings() =>
        new[] { Zone("S", 0, 0, 30, 10), Zone("N", 0, 20, 30, 30), Zone("W", 0, 10, 10, 20), Zone("E", 20, 10, 30, 20) };

    private static bool OnCourt(WallSurface wall) =>
        new[] { wall.Start, wall.End }.All(p => p.X is >= 10 and <= 20 && p.Y is >= 10 and <= 20);

    private static LayoutZone Zone(string id, double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1) => new(new ZoneId(id), Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, y0, x1, y1));

    private Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> Build(Polygon2 footprint, params LayoutZone[] zones) => _builder.Build(footprint, zones, 3.0);
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Layout/LayoutSurfaceBuilder.cs` (full file)**

`CheckBoundaryCovered` is added and called after `Classify`; the class summary names the check. Nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Layout;

/// <summary>A zone footprint to build surfaces for.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone floor polygon.</param>
public sealed record LayoutZone(ZoneId Id, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>
/// Builds the surfaces of a single-storey layout: walls split wherever another zone's vertex touches them, paired across zones
/// (interzone), or on the plan boundary (outdoors), then merged into maximal collinear runs; plus one unresolved floor and ceiling per zone.
/// Edges that are neither shared nor on the boundary indicate gaps or overlaps and are errors, and so is a boundary edge that zones do not cover
/// outdoors over its full length. The boundary includes the footprint's holes: the edges of a court are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LayoutSurfaceBuilder
{
    private readonly ToleranceSettings _tolerances;

    /// <summary>Creates the builder.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances; <see cref="ToleranceSettings.Distance"/> is the vertex matching grid.</param>
    public LayoutSurfaceBuilder(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        _tolerances = tolerances;
    }

    /// <summary>Builds walls (without windows), floors, and ceilings.</summary>
    /// <param name="footprint">Plan boundary; walls on it are outdoors.</param>
    /// <param name="zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
    /// <param name="height">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Surfaces grouped by zone (walls, floor, ceiling), or errors for gaps and overlaps.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> Build(Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, double height)
    {
        var grid = new VertexGrid(_tolerances.Distance);
        foreach (Point2 p in footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r).Concat(zones.SelectMany(z => z.Footprint.Rings.SelectMany(r => r))))
        {
            grid.Register(p);
        }

        GridPoint[] vertices = grid.Keys.OrderBy(k => k.X).ThenBy(k => k.Y).ToArray();
        var segments = new List<Segment>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            int ringIndex = 0;
            foreach (IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring in zones[zoneIndex].Footprint.Rings)
            {
                AddRingSegments(grid, vertices, zoneIndex, ringIndex++, ring, segments);
            }
        }

        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        Classify(grid, footprint, zones, segments, diagnostics);
        CheckBoundaryCovered(grid, footprint, segments, diagnostics);
        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(diagnostics);
        }

        var surfaces = new List<Surface>();
        for (int zoneIndex = 0; zoneIndex < zones.Count; zoneIndex++)
        {
            LayoutZone zone = zones[zoneIndex];
            int wallNumber = 1;
            foreach (IGrouping<int, Segment> ring in segments.Where(s => s.Zone == zoneIndex).GroupBy(s => s.Ring))
            {
                foreach (List<Segment> run in MergeRuns(ring.ToList()))
                {
                    Segment first = run[0];
                    surfaces.Add(new WallSurface(
                        new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/W{wallNumber++}"),
                        zone.Id,
                        grid.Original(first.A),
                        grid.Original(run[run.Count - 1].B),
                        elevation: 0.0,
                        height,
                        first.Boundary,
                        first.Adjacent is int adjacent ? zones[adjacent].Id : null,
                        Array.Empty<Window>()));
                }
            }

            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/F"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Floor, zone.Footprint, 0.0, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
            surfaces.Add(new HorizontalSurface(new SurfaceId($"{zone.Id.Value}/C"), zone.Id, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, zone.Footprint, height, BoundaryCondition.Unresolved, null));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<Surface>>(surfaces);
    }

    private static void AddRingSegments(VertexGrid grid, GridPoint[] vertices, int zone, int ring, IReadOnlyList<Point2> points, List<Segment> segments)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
        {
            GridPoint a = grid.Key(points[i]);
            GridPoint b = grid.Key(points[(i + 1) % points.Count]);
            if (a == b)
            {
                continue;
            }

            GridPoint[] cuts = vertices
                .Where(v => v != a && v != b && grid.IsStrictlyInside(a, b, v))
                .OrderBy(v => VertexGrid.Parameter(a, b, v))
                .ToArray();
            GridPoint previous = a;
            foreach (GridPoint cut in cuts.Concat(new[] { b }))
            {
                segments.Add(new Segment(zone, ring, previous, cut));
                previous = cut;
            }
        }
    }

    private static void Classify(VertexGrid grid, Polygon2 footprint, IReadOnlyList<LayoutZone> zones, List<Segment> segments, List<Diagnostic> diagnostics)
    {
        var byDirection = segments.GroupBy(s => (s.A, s.B)).ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.ToList());
        GridPoint[][] boundaryRings = footprint.Rings.Select(r => r.Select(grid.Key).ToArray()).ToArray();
        foreach (Segment segment in segments)
        {
            string subject = zones[segment.Zone].Id.Value;
            if (byDirection[(segment.A, segment.B)].Count > 1)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.OverlappingZones, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is used by more than one zone in the same direction.", subject));
                continue;
            }

            if (byDirection.TryGetValue((segment.B, segment.A), out List<Segment>? opposite))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Interzone;
                segment.Adjacent = opposite[0].Zone;
            }
            else if (boundaryRings.Any(ring => OnRing(grid, ring, segment.A, segment.B)))
            {
                segment.Boundary = BoundaryCondition.Outdoors;
            }
            else
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge, $"Edge {Describe(grid, segment)} is neither shared with another zone nor on the plan boundary (gap or overlap).", subject));
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Every edge of the plan boundary, outer ring and holes, must be covered over its full length by outdoor zone edges (within the relative
    /// tolerance, as façade coverage is compared). Edge matching alone misses a zone lying outside the footprint whose every edge is shared with
    /// another zone, such as a zone filling a court: the court's edges are then interzone instead of outdoors.
    /// </summary>
    private void CheckBoundaryCovered(VertexGrid grid, Polygon2 footprint, List<Segment> segments, List<Diagnostic> diagnostics)
    {
        Segment[] outdoor = segments.Where(s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        foreach (GridPoint[] ring in footprint.Rings.Select(r => r.Select(grid.Key).ToArray()))
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ring.Length; i++)
            {
                GridPoint p = ring[i];
                GridPoint q = ring[(i + 1) % ring.Length];
                if (p == q)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                double length = grid.Original(p).DistanceTo(grid.Original(q));
                double covered = outdoor.Where(s => grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, s.A) && grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, s.B)).Sum(s => grid.Original(s.A).DistanceTo(grid.Original(s.B)));
                if (!_tolerances.AreaEquals(length, covered))
                {
                    Point2 a = grid.Original(p);
                    Point2 b = grid.Original(q);
                    diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                        DiagnosticCodes.UnmatchedEdge,
                        $"Plan boundary edge ({a.X:0.###}, {a.Y:0.###})-({b.X:0.###}, {b.Y:0.###}) is the outdoor edge of zones over {covered:0.###} m of its {length:0.###} m (a zone lies outside the footprint, such as in a court, or the zones leave a gap)."));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private IEnumerable<List<Segment>> MergeRuns(List<Segment> ring)
    {
        int start = Enumerable.Range(0, ring.Count).FirstOrDefault(i => !CanMerge(ring[(i + ring.Count - 1) % ring.Count], ring[i]));
        List<Segment>? run = null;
        for (int k = 0; k < ring.Count; k++)
        {
            Segment segment = ring[(start + k) % ring.Count];
            if (run is not null && CanMerge(run[run.Count - 1], segment))
            {
                run.Add(segment);
                continue;
            }

            if (run is not null)
            {
                yield return run;
            }

            run = new List<Segment> { segment };
        }

        if (run is not null)
        {
            yield return run;
        }
    }

    private bool CanMerge(Segment previous, Segment next)
    {
        if (previous.B != next.A || previous.Boundary != next.Boundary || previous.Adjacent != next.Adjacent)
        {
            return false;
        }

        double ax = previous.B.X - previous.A.X;
        double ay = previous.B.Y - previous.A.Y;
        double bx = next.B.X - next.A.X;
        double by = next.B.Y - next.A.Y;
        double lengths = Math.Sqrt((ax * ax) + (ay * ay)) * Math.Sqrt((bx * bx) + (by * by));
        double sine = ((ax * by) - (ay * bx)) / lengths;
        double cosine = ((ax * bx) + (ay * by)) / lengths;
        return cosine > 0 && Math.Abs(sine) <= Math.Sin(_tolerances.Angle);
    }

    private static bool OnRing(VertexGrid grid, GridPoint[] ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < ring.Length; i++)
        {
            GridPoint p = ring[i];
            GridPoint q = ring[(i + 1) % ring.Length];
            if (grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, a) && grid.IsOnSegment(p, q, b))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static string Describe(VertexGrid grid, Segment segment)
    {
        Point2 a = grid.Original(segment.A);
        Point2 b = grid.Original(segment.B);
        return $"({a.X:0.###}, {a.Y:0.###})-({b.X:0.###}, {b.Y:0.###})";
    }

    private sealed class Segment
    {
        public Segment(int zone, int ring, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
        {
            Zone = zone;
            Ring = ring;
            A = a;
            B = b;
        }

        public int Zone { get; }

        public int Ring { get; }

        public GridPoint A { get; }

        public GridPoint B { get; }

        public BoundaryCondition Boundary { get; set; } = BoundaryCondition.Unresolved;

        public int? Adjacent { get; set; }
    }
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

No production code changes.

- [x] **Step 1: Create the ring plan — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RingPlan.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The simplest footprint with a hole: four dwelling wings of one depth around a square court, south and north full width, west and east between
/// them. Used to test every pipeline step on a court before the enclosed courtyard generator exists.
/// </summary>
internal static class RingPlan
{
    /// <summary>A plan of four <paramref name="wing"/> deep wings around a <paramref name="court"/> × <paramref name="court"/> court, 3 m storeys.</summary>
    public static IGeneratedPlan Generate(double wing = 10.0, double court = 10.0) =>
        new RingGenerator().Generate(new RingParameters(wing, court), new RingPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit)).Value;

    /// <summary>The plan moved by one of the placements used for rotated plans.</summary>
    public static IGeneratedPlan Placed(double rotationDegrees, double dx, double dy, double wing = 10.0, double court = 10.0) =>
        PlanTransform.Apply(Generate(wing, court), rotationDegrees, dx, dy).Value;

    private sealed record RingParameters(double Wing, double Court) : PlanParameters(3.0, 0.0);

    private sealed record RingPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[] { new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit) };
    }

    private sealed class RingGenerator : PlanGenerator<RingParameters, RingPresets>
    {
        public RingGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(RingGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(RingParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(RingParameters p)
        {
            double d = p.Wing;
            double side = (2.0 * d) + p.Court;
            PlannedZone Zone(string id, double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1) =>
                new(new ZoneId(id), id, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, y0, x1, y1));
            Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, side, side).Outer, new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(d, d, d + p.Court, d + p.Court).Outer }).Value;
            return new PlanLayout(footprint, new[]
            {
                Zone("S", 0, 0, side, d),
                Zone("N", 0, d + p.Court, side, side),
                Zone("W", 0, d, d, d + p.Court),
                Zone("E", d + p.Court, d, side, d + p.Court),
            });
        }

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(RingParameters p) => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RingFootprintTests.cs` (full file)**

10 cases: `TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows` (four court walls, of `S`, `N`, `W`, `E`, each with one window; 800 m²), and at each of the three placements `NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls`, `SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt` (one zone with one hole, 800 m², 8 outdoor walls each with one centred window, 4 of them on the court), and `SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole`; every floor validates.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Footprints with a hole through the pipeline (S8.3) on <see cref="RingPlan"/>: a 30 m × 30 m footprint of four 10 m deep dwelling wings around a
/// 10 m × 10 m court. The court is outside the footprint; its edges are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RingFootprintTests
{
    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 }, new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacementIndices() => Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length).Select(i => new object[] { i });

    [Fact]
    public void TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(plan.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, court.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls(int placement)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(placement);

        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
        AssertFloorValidates(floor);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone ring = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(ring.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(800.0, ring.FloorArea), $"Area {ring.FloorArea}");
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone floor = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(floor.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        return placement == 0 ? RingPlan.Generate() : RingPlan.Placed(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    }

    /// <summary>The outdoor walls on the court's edges, found in the plan frame before the placement.</summary>
    private static WallSurface[] CourtWalls(IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces, int placement = 0)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        var back = new PlanMotion(-p[0], 0.0, 0.0);
        Point2 Unplace(Point2 q) => back.Apply(new Point2(q.X - p[1], q.Y - p[2]));
        bool OnCourt(Point2 q) => Unplace(q) is var u && u.X is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6 && u.Y is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6;
        return surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w.Start) && OnCourt(w.End)).ToArray();
    }

    private static void AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IFloor floor, int outdoorWalls, int placement)
    {
        WallSurface[] outdoor = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(outdoorWalls, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
    }

    private static void AssertFloorValidates(IFloor floor)
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }
}
```

### Task 3: *Perimeter Core* zones the court façades of footprints with holes

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (S3 file, last changed in S8.2)
- Test: modify `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RingFootprintTests.cs` (Task 2), `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/SimplifierTests.cs` (S3 file, last changed in S8.2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double depth)` (S3; ADR-008: each footprint edge owns the trapezoid between the edge and its mitred inward offset, trapezoids of one orientation bin united into `P-<Bin>`, the offset ring the core `CORE`; S8.2: the `PlanTooNarrow` message ends with the D-087 precondition), `Polygon2.SignedArea(IReadOnlyList<Point2>)`, `PolygonOps.Difference(Polygon2, IEnumerable<Polygon2>)`, `PolygonOps.Union`, `Orientation.OutwardAzimuth`, `DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow`, `DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported` (S3); `RingPlan` and the helpers of Task 2.
- Produces: `PerimeterCore` offsets every ring of the footprint with `Mitre` (`Offset`), requires each offset ring to keep its edges' directions (`KeepsDirections`), its ring's orientation (positive signed area for the outer ring, negative for a hole), and to be a valid polygon, and the core `Polygon2.Create(offset outer, offset holes)` to be valid; with holes, clipping the offset holes from the offset outer ring must give one polygon of the core's area, else `PlanTooNarrow` with `Perimeter depth {d} m leaves no valid core between the outer and the court façades: ` and the precondition. The trapezoids of the outer ring give `P-<Bin>` (*Perimeter <Bin>*) as before; those of the holes, whose bin is the direction the court façade faces, are united per bin into `P-Court-<Bin>` (*Perimeter Court <Bin>*, suffixes `-1`, `-2` as for the outer zones), listed after the outer zones; the core `CORE` has a hole for each court. `NotSupported` is no longer emitted by *Perimeter Core* (the constant stays in `DiagnosticCodes`). Footprints without holes are zoned exactly as before.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RingFootprintTests.cs` (full file)**

Nine new cases: `PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacadesAroundARingCore` (zones `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, `P-Court-North`, `P-Court-East`, `P-Court-South`, `P-Court-West`, `CORE`; outer trapezoids (60 − 2d)·d/2 and court trapezoids (20 + 2d)·d/2 with d = 4.57 m; the core (30 − 2d)² − (10 + 2d)² with one hole; 800 m² in all), `CourtPerimeterZonesHoldTheCourtFacadesAndTheCoreHasNone`, `PerimeterCoreOfARingValidates` at the three placements, `WingsNotWiderThanTwiceTheDepthAreTooNarrow` (wings of 8 m and 9.14 m around a 10 m court, and 4 m around a 30 m court), and `AWingJustWiderThanTwiceTheDepthIsZoned` (9.2 m wings: a 0.06 m wide core ring). `using System;` and the constant `PerimeterDepth` are added; the tests of Task 2 are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Footprints with a hole through the pipeline (S8.3) on <see cref="RingPlan"/>: a 30 m × 30 m footprint of four 10 m deep dwelling wings around a
/// 10 m × 10 m court. The court is outside the footprint; its edges are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RingFootprintTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 }, new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacementIndices() => Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length).Select(i => new object[] { i });

    [Fact]
    public void TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(plan.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, court.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls(int placement)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(placement);

        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
        AssertFloorValidates(floor);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone ring = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(ring.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(800.0, ring.FloorArea), $"Area {ring.FloorArea}");
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone floor = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(floor.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacadesAroundARingCore()
    {
        // Bisector trapezoids on the outer ring and on the court's ring (ADR-008); the court's façades get their own perimeter zones, named by the
        // direction they face, and the core is what is left between them: a ring around the court.
        const double d = PerimeterDepth;
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, d).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "P-Court-North", "P-Court-East", "P-Court-South", "P-Court-West", "CORE" },
            floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal("Perimeter Court North", floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-Court-North").Name);
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-") && !z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((30.0 + 30.0 - (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((10.0 + 10.0 + (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(30.0 - (2 * d), 2) - Math.Pow(10.0 + (2 * d), 2), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.Single(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CourtPerimeterZonesHoldTheCourtFacadesAndTheCoreHasNone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("P-Court-North", OrientationBin.North), ("P-Court-East", OrientationBin.East), ("P-Court-South", OrientationBin.South), ("P-Court-West", OrientationBin.West) },
            court.Select(w => (w.Zone.Value, Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))));
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), w => Assert.Contains(w, court));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(floor, 8, 0);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void PerimeterCoreOfARingValidates(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(9, result.Value.Zones.Count);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(8.0, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(9.14, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(4.0, 30.0)]
    public void WingsNotWiderThanTwiceTheDepthAreTooNarrow(double wing, double court)
    {
        // D-087 applies to every wing between the outer and the court façades: the outer and court perimeter bands would overlap.
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(wing, court));

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.EndsWith("every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingJustWiderThanTwiceTheDepthIsZoned()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(9.2, 10.0));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(0.06 * 4 * (10.0 + (2 * 4.57) + 0.06), result.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
    }

    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        return placement == 0 ? RingPlan.Generate() : RingPlan.Placed(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    }

    /// <summary>The outdoor walls on the court's edges, found in the plan frame before the placement.</summary>
    private static WallSurface[] CourtWalls(IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces, int placement = 0)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        var back = new PlanMotion(-p[0], 0.0, 0.0);
        Point2 Unplace(Point2 q) => back.Apply(new Point2(q.X - p[1], q.Y - p[2]));
        bool OnCourt(Point2 q) => Unplace(q) is var u && u.X is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6 && u.Y is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6;
        return surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w.Start) && OnCourt(w.End)).ToArray();
    }

    private static void AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IFloor floor, int outdoorWalls, int placement)
    {
        WallSurface[] outdoor = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(outdoorWalls, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
    }

    private static void AssertFloorValidates(IFloor floor)
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }
}
```

`FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet` is removed: it asserted `NotSupported` for a ring footprint, which this task replaces by zoning it (D-084). Every other test is unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Loads;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.Linear;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SimplifierTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsConnectedZonesOfTheSameType()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "Corridor-1", "DwellingUnit-1", "DwellingUnit-2" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Zone south = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1");
        Assert.Equal(160.0, south.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "US1", "US2" }, south.SourceZones.Select(s => s.Value));
        Assert.Equal(5.0, south.Program.Find(LoadType.Lighting, LoadBasis.PerFloorArea)!.Value, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergedZonesLoseTheirSharedPartition()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces, s => s.Zone == s.AdjacentZone);
        Assert.Equal(2, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Zone.Value == "DwellingUnit-1" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesZonesMeetingAtTJunctionsOnARotatedPlan()
    {
        // The point plate's dwellings meet each other and the core at T-junctions; rotated off the axes, a plain union of their footprints leaves
        // slits where clipping-grid rounding moves a T-junction off its edge, so the zones are united as tiles (ADR-013).
        IGeneratedPlan plate = new PointPlateGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(PointPlateParameters.Default, new PointPlatePresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(plate, 30.0, 5.3, -2.1).Value;

        Result<IFloor> floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair-1", "DwellingUnit-1" }, floor.Value.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Polygon2 ring = floor.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "DwellingUnit-1").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Single(ring.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(4 * 112.0, ring.Area), $"Area {ring.Area}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreHasFourPerimeterZonesAndACore()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "CORE" }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)) * (18.0 - (2 * PerimeterDepth)), floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterSouthHasOneCentredWindowWithTheSouthFacadeGlazing()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan()).Value;

        WallSurface south = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == "P-South" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Window window = Assert.Single(south.Windows);
        Assert.Equal(1.2 + 9.0 + 9.0, window.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal((24.0 - window.Width) / 2.0, window.Offset, 9);
        Assert.Equal((3.0 - window.Height) / 2.0, window.SillHeight, 9);
        Assert.Equal(window.Width / 24.0, window.Height / 3.0, 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterDepthTooLargeIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 10.0).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreAnActionableError()
    {
        // 8 m deep wings around an open court: the 4.57 m perimeters of a wing's two façades would overlap (ADR-008 precondition).
        IGeneratedPlan court = new OpenCourtGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances)
            .Generate(OpenCourtParameters.Default with { Depth = 8.0 }, new OpenCourtPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair)).Value;

        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(court);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core for this footprint: every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0)]
    [InlineData(-1.0)]
    public void NonPositivePerimeterDepthIsAnError(double depth)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, depth).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan());

        Assert.Contains(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorHasOnlyOutdoorWalls()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(floor.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Mixed, zone.SpaceType);
        Assert.Equal(6, zone.SourceZones.Count);
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, w.Boundary));
        Assert.Equal(4, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SetpointsAreFloorAreaWeightedAndRecorded()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));

        Thermostat thermostat = floor.Zones[0].Program.Thermostat!;
        Assert.Equal(21.0, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint[0], 9);
        Assert.Equal(Lod.Core.Aggregation.AggregationMethod.FloorAreaWeighted, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation!.Method);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(new ZoneId("ST"), thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources);
        Assert.Equal(5, thermostat.HeatingSetpoint.Aggregation.Sources.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 1)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 2)]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 1)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 2)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 3)]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore", 4)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 1)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor", 2)]
    public void EverySimplifierSatisfiesTheInvariantsForRandomPlans(string simplifier, int seed)
    {
        LinearPlanParameters parameters = RandomParameters(new Random(seed));

        IFloor floor = Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Pipeline.Plan(parameters)).Value;
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MergingTheUnconditionedStairEnlargesConditionedAreaAndIsReported()
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances)));

        CheckResult conditioned = report.Checks.Single(c => c.Check == "ConditionedFloorArea");
        Assert.False(conditioned.Passed);
        Assert.False(conditioned.Enforced);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAnUncoveredFacade()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Surface[] withoutSouthWall = floor.Surfaces.Where(s => !(s is WallSurface w && w.Start.Y == 0.0 && w.End.Y == 0.0)).ToArray();
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, floor.Zones, withoutSouthWall, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "FacadeCoverage" && !c.Passed && c.Enforced);
        Assert.False(report.Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NoSimplificationPassesValidation()
    {
        Assert.True(new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(Pipeline.DetailedFloor()).Passed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ValidationCatchesAChangedProgram()
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Floor(new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances));
        Zone zone = floor.Zones[0];
        var tampered = new Zone(zone.Id, zone.Name, zone.SpaceType, zone.Parts, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.Program, zone.SourceZones);
        var broken = new Floor(floor.Source, new[] { tampered }, floor.Surfaces, floor.Mapping, floor.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(broken);

        Assert.False(report.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Installed.Lighting" && !c.Passed);
        Assert.Contains(report.Checks, c => c.Check == "Traceability" && !c.Passed);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void SimplifiersAreDeterministic(string simplifier)
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SemanticMerge")]
    [InlineData("PerimeterCore")]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloor")]
    public void CanonicalSnapshots(string simplifier)
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Pipeline.Floor(Simplifier(simplifier))), $"simplifier-{simplifier}");
    }

    private static IPlanSimplifier Simplifier(string name) => name switch
    {
        "SemanticMerge" => new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        "PerimeterCore" => new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth),
        "SingleZonePerFloor" => new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances),
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(name), name, "Unknown simplifier."),
    };

    private static LinearPlanParameters RandomParameters(Random random) => new(
        Length: 18.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 42.0),
        UnitDepth: 6.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 4.0),
        CorridorWidth: 1.5 + (random.NextDouble() * 1.5),
        TargetUnitWidth: 5.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 7.0),
        StairLength: 3.0 + (random.NextDouble() * 3.0),
        FloorHeight: 2.8 + (random.NextDouble() * 0.7),
        OrientationDegrees: random.NextDouble() * 360.0);
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Simplification;

/// <summary>
/// Z1: merges zones of the same space type that are connected through shared walls. Each connected group becomes one zone, the union of its
/// footprints as tiles (<see cref="PolygonOps.UnionTiles"/>), so zones meeting at T-junctions unite exactly on rotated plans; the union may have
/// holes, where the group surrounds zones of another type.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SemanticMerge : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SemanticMerge);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        Dictionary<ZoneId, Zone> byId = plan.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id);
        ILookup<ZoneId, ZoneId> neighbours = plan.Surfaces
            .Where(s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .ToLookup(s => s.Zone, s => s.AdjacentZone!.Value);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var visited = new HashSet<ZoneId>();
        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        var counters = new Dictionary<SpaceType, int>();
        foreach (Zone seed in plan.Zones.Where(z => !visited.Contains(z.Id)))
        {
            var group = new List<Zone>();
            var queue = new Queue<Zone>(new[] { seed });
            visited.Add(seed.Id);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                Zone current = queue.Dequeue();
                group.Add(current);
                foreach (ZoneId next in neighbours[current.Id].Where(n => !visited.Contains(n) && byId[n].SpaceType == seed.SpaceType))
                {
                    visited.Add(next);
                    queue.Enqueue(byId[next]);
                }
            }

            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> union = ops.UnionTiles(group.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint).ToArray());
            if (union.Count != 1)
            {
                return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.DisconnectedGroup,
                    $"Merged group {string.Join(",", group.Select(z => z.Id.Value))} is not one polygon.",
                    seed.Id.Value));
            }

            int number = counters.TryGetValue(seed.SpaceType, out int n) ? n + 1 : 1;
            counters[seed.SpaceType] = number;
            targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{seed.SpaceType}-{number}"), $"{seed.SpaceType} {number}", seed.SpaceType, union[0]));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}

/// <summary>
/// Z2: perimeter zones of a configurable depth plus a core (ADR-008). Each footprint edge owns the trapezoid between the edge and its inward
/// mitred offset, so corners are split along the bisectors; trapezoids in the same orientation bin are merged. A footprint with holes (courts) is
/// offset on every ring: the edges of the holes face into their courts and give court perimeter zones <c>P-Court-&lt;Bin&gt;</c>, separate from
/// the outer façades' zones, and the core is what lies between the offset rings, a ring around each court.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PerimeterCore : PlanSimplifier
{
    private readonly double _depth;

    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="perimeterDepth">Perimeter zone depth, m.</param>
    public PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings tolerances, double perimeterDepth)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
        _depth = perimeterDepth;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(PerimeterCore);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        if (!(_depth > 0) || double.IsInfinity(_depth))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Perimeter depth must be positive, got {_depth}.");
        }

        // Every ring keeps the floor on its left (outer ring counter-clockwise, holes clockwise), so one mitre rule offsets each ring into the floor.
        IReadOnlyList<Point2>[] rings = plan.Footprint.Rings.ToArray();
        Point2[][] offsets = rings.Select(Offset).ToArray();
        Result<Polygon2> core = Polygon2.Create(offsets[0], offsets.Skip(1));
        bool ringsValid = Enumerable.Range(0, rings.Length).All(r =>
            KeepsDirections(rings[r], offsets[r]) && (r == 0 ? Polygon2.SignedArea(offsets[r]) > 0 : Polygon2.SignedArea(offsets[r]) < 0) && Polygon2.Create(offsets[r]).IsSuccess);
        if (!core.IsSuccess || !ringsValid)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core for this footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        if (rings.Length > 1)
        {
            // The offset holes must lie inside the offset outer ring and apart from each other; otherwise a wing between the outer and a court façade,
            // or between two courts, is not wider than twice the depth and the perimeter bands overlap.
            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> between = ops.Difference(Polygon2.Create(offsets[0]).Value, offsets.Skip(1).Select(h => Polygon2.Create(h).Value));
            if (between.Count != 1 || !Tolerances.AreaEquals(core.Value.Area, between.Sum(p => p.Area)))
            {
                return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core between the outer and the court façades: {Precondition()}");
            }
        }

        var trapezoids = new List<(bool Court, OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)>();
        for (int r = 0; r < rings.Length; r++)
        {
            IReadOnlyList<Point2> v = rings[r];
            Point2[] offset = offsets[r];
            int n = v.Count;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                Polygon2 shape = Polygon2.Create(new[] { v[i], v[(i + 1) % n], offset[(i + 1) % n], offset[i] }).Value;
                trapezoids.Add((r > 0, Orientation.Bin(Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(v[i], v[(i + 1) % n]) + plan.OrientationDegrees), shape));
            }
        }

        double tiled = trapezoids.Sum(t => t.Shape.Area) + core.Value.Area;
        if (!Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, tiled))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter zones and core cover {tiled:0.###} m² of a {plan.Footprint.Area:0.###} m² footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        foreach (bool court in new[] { false, true })
        {
            string id = court ? "P-Court-" : "P-";
            string name = court ? "Perimeter Court " : "Perimeter ";
            foreach (IGrouping<OrientationBin, (bool Court, OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)> bin in trapezoids.Where(t => t.Court == court).GroupBy(t => t.Bin).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
            {
                IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> merged = ops.Union(bin.Select(t => t.Shape));
                for (int k = 0; k < merged.Count; k++)
                {
                    string suffix = merged.Count == 1 ? string.Empty : $"-{k + 1}";
                    targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{id}{bin.Key}{suffix}"), $"{name}{bin.Key}{suffix}", SpaceType.Mixed, merged[k]));
                }
            }
        }

        targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId("CORE"), "Core", SpaceType.Mixed, core.Value));
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() =>
        new[] { new KeyValuePair<string, string>("PerimeterDepth", Provenance.Format(_depth)) };

    /// <summary>The precondition a <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow"/> plan breaks, with what the user can do (ADR-008).</summary>
    private string Precondition() =>
        $"every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth ({2.0 * _depth} m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.";

    /// <summary>The mitred inward offset of every vertex of a ring, so offset vertex <c>i</c> belongs to ring vertex <c>i</c> (ADR-008).</summary>
    private Point2[] Offset(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring)
    {
        int n = ring.Count;
        return Enumerable.Range(0, n).Select(i => Mitre(ring[(i + n - 1) % n], ring[i], ring[(i + 1) % n])).ToArray();
    }

    /// <summary>Whether every offset edge keeps the direction of its ring edge; a reversed edge means the depth is too large for the edge.</summary>
    private static bool KeepsDirections(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring, Point2[] offset)
    {
        int n = ring.Count;
        return Enumerable.Range(0, n).All(i =>
        {
            Point2 a = ring[i];
            Point2 b = ring[(i + 1) % n];
            Point2 c = offset[i];
            Point2 d = offset[(i + 1) % n];
            return ((b.X - a.X) * (d.X - c.X)) + ((b.Y - a.Y) * (d.Y - c.Y)) > 0;
        });
    }

    private Point2 Mitre(Point2 previous, Point2 vertex, Point2 next)
    {
        (double X, double Y) a = InwardNormal(previous, vertex);
        (double X, double Y) b = InwardNormal(vertex, next);
        double scale = _depth / (1.0 + (a.X * b.X) + (a.Y * b.Y));
        return new Point2(vertex.X + ((a.X + b.X) * scale), vertex.Y + ((a.Y + b.Y) * scale));
    }

    private static (double X, double Y) InwardNormal(Point2 from, Point2 to)
    {
        double length = from.DistanceTo(to);
        return (-(to.Y - from.Y) / length, (to.X - from.X) / length);
    }

    private static Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> Fail(string code, string message) =>
        Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(code, message));
}

/// <summary>Z3: the whole floor as one zone.</summary>
public sealed class SingleZonePerFloor : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SingleZonePerFloor(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SingleZonePerFloor);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        SpaceType[] types = plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct().ToArray();
        SpaceType type = types.Length == 1 ? types[0] : SpaceType.Mixed;
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(new[] { new TargetZone(new ZoneId("FLOOR"), "Floor", type, plan.Footprint) });
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: test support `Pipeline.Simplifier(string name)`, `Pipeline.Aggregator(string name)` (S4.2); `FloorEntry(IFloor, int)`, `IFloorAggregator.Aggregate`, `BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings).Validate`, `Totals.Of(zones, surfaces, orientationDegrees)` (`GroundArea`, `RoofArea`, `ExposedFloorArea`), `PolygonOps.IntersectionArea`, `HorizontalSurface`, `DiagnosticSeverity.Warning`; Tasks 2 and 3.

No production code changes.
