```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Footprints with a hole through the pipeline (S8.3) on <see cref="RingPlan"/>: a 30 m × 30 m footprint of four 10 m deep dwelling wings around a
/// 10 m × 10 m court. The court is outside the footprint; its edges are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RingFootprintTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 }, new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacementIndices() => Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length).Select(i => new object[] { i });

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator, placement };

    [Fact]
    public void TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(plan.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, court.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls(int placement)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(placement);

        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
        AssertFloorValidates(floor);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone ring = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(ring.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(800.0, ring.FloorArea), $"Area {ring.FloorArea}");
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone floor = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(floor.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacadesAroundARingCore()
    {
        // Bisector trapezoids on the outer ring and on the court's ring (ADR-008); the court's façades get their own perimeter zones, named by the
        // direction they face, and the core is what is left between them: a ring around the court.
        const double d = PerimeterDepth;
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, d).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "P-Court-North", "P-Court-East", "P-Court-South", "P-Court-West", "CORE" },
            floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal("Perimeter Court North", floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-Court-North").Name);
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-") && !z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((30.0 + 30.0 - (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((10.0 + 10.0 + (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(30.0 - (2 * d), 2) - Math.Pow(10.0 + (2 * d), 2), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.Single(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CourtPerimeterZonesHoldTheCourtFacadesAndTheCoreHasNone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("P-Court-North", OrientationBin.North), ("P-Court-East", OrientationBin.East), ("P-Court-South", OrientationBin.South), ("P-Court-West", OrientationBin.West) },
            court.Select(w => (w.Zone.Value, Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))));
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), w => Assert.Contains(w, court));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(floor, 8, 0);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void PerimeterCoreOfARingValidates(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(9, result.Value.Zones.Count);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(8.0, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(9.14, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(4.0, 30.0)]
    public void WingsNotWiderThanTwiceTheDepthAreTooNarrow(double wing, double court)
    {
        // D-087 applies to every wing between the outer and the court façades: the outer and court perimeter bands would overlap.
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(wing, court));

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.EndsWith("every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingJustWiderThanTwiceTheDepthIsZoned()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(9.2, 10.0));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(0.06 * 4 * (10.0 + (2 * 4.57) + 0.06), result.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryAggregatorBuildsAndValidatesARingBuilding(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Plan(placement)).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor));

        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack", 5)]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", 3)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", 5)]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", 5)]
    public void TheCourtHasNoGroundSlabAndNoRoof(string aggregator, int storeysModelled)
    {
        // The court is outside the footprint: ground and roof are the footprint's 800 m², not the outer rectangle's 900 m².
        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value)).Value;

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces.ToArray(), building.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.GroundArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.RoofArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 6);
        Assert.All(building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Interzone), h => Assert.Equal(0.0, Overlap(h.Polygon, Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 10, 20, 20)), 9));
        Assert.Equal(storeysModelled, building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).Select(p => p.Elevation).Distinct().Count());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", "E0,E1,E2")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", "BUILDING")]
    public void SingleZoneMethodsRebuildTheCourtWallsOfEveryStoreyWithCentredWindows(string aggregator, string zones)
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.All(building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts), p => Assert.Single(p.Footprint.Holes));
        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(building.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(5 * 4, court.Length);
        double sourceCourtGlazing = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(5 * sourceCourtGlazing, court.Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    private static FloorEntry[] Entries(IFloor floor) => new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) };

    private static double Overlap(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => new PolygonOps(Pipeline.Tolerances).IntersectionArea(a, b);

    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        return placement == 0 ? RingPlan.Generate() : RingPlan.Placed(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    }

    /// <summary>The outdoor walls on the court's edges, found in the plan frame before the placement.</summary>
    private static WallSurface[] CourtWalls(IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces, int placement = 0)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        var back = new PlanMotion(-p[0], 0.0, 0.0);
        Point2 Unplace(Point2 q) => back.Apply(new Point2(q.X - p[1], q.Y - p[2]));
        bool OnCourt(Point2 q) => Unplace(q) is var u && u.X is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6 && u.Y is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6;
        return surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w.Start) && OnCourt(w.End)).ToArray();
    }

    private static void AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IFloor floor, int outdoorWalls, int placement)
    {
        WallSurface[] outdoor = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(outdoorWalls, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
    }

    private static void AssertFloorValidates(IFloor floor)
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Footprints with a hole through the pipeline (S8.3) on <see cref="RingPlan"/>: a 30 m × 30 m footprint of four 10 m deep dwelling wings around a
/// 10 m × 10 m court. The court is outside the footprint; its edges are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RingFootprintTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 }, new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacementIndices() => Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length).Select(i => new object[] { i });

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator, placement };

    [Fact]
    public void TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(plan.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, court.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls(int placement)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(placement);

        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
        AssertFloorValidates(floor);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone ring = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(ring.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(800.0, ring.FloorArea), $"Area {ring.FloorArea}");
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone floor = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(floor.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacadesAroundARingCore()
    {
        // Bisector trapezoids on the outer ring and on the court's ring (ADR-008); the court's façades get their own perimeter zones, named by the
        // direction they face, and the core is what is left between them: a ring around the court.
        const double d = PerimeterDepth;
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, d).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "P-Court-North", "P-Court-East", "P-Court-South", "P-Court-West", "CORE" },
            floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal("Perimeter Court North", floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-Court-North").Name);
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-") && !z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((30.0 + 30.0 - (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((10.0 + 10.0 + (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(30.0 - (2 * d), 2) - Math.Pow(10.0 + (2 * d), 2), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.Single(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CourtPerimeterZonesHoldTheCourtFacadesAndTheCoreHasNone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("P-Court-North", OrientationBin.North), ("P-Court-East", OrientationBin.East), ("P-Court-South", OrientationBin.South), ("P-Court-West", OrientationBin.West) },
            court.Select(w => (w.Zone.Value, Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))));
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), w => Assert.Contains(w, court));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(floor, 8, 0);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void PerimeterCoreOfARingValidates(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(9, result.Value.Zones.Count);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(8.0, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(9.14, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(4.0, 30.0)]
    public void WingsNotWiderThanTwiceTheDepthAreTooNarrow(double wing, double court)
    {
        // D-087 applies to every wing between the outer and the court façades: the outer and court perimeter bands would overlap.
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(wing, court));

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.EndsWith("every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingJustWiderThanTwiceTheDepthIsZoned()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(9.2, 10.0));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(0.06 * 4 * (10.0 + (2 * 4.57) + 0.06), result.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryAggregatorBuildsAndValidatesARingBuilding(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Plan(placement)).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor));

        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack", 5)]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", 3)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", 5)]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", 5)]
    public void TheCourtHasNoGroundSlabAndNoRoof(string aggregator, int storeysModelled)
    {
        // The court is outside the footprint: ground and roof are the footprint's 800 m², not the outer rectangle's 900 m².
        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value)).Value;

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces.ToArray(), building.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.GroundArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.RoofArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 6);
        Assert.All(building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Interzone), h => Assert.Equal(0.0, Overlap(h.Polygon, Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 10, 20, 20)), 9));
        Assert.Equal(storeysModelled, building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).Select(p => p.Elevation).Distinct().Count());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", "E0,E1,E2")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", "BUILDING")]
    public void SingleZoneMethodsRebuildTheCourtWallsOfEveryStoreyWithCentredWindows(string aggregator, string zones)
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.All(building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts), p => Assert.Single(p.Footprint.Holes));
        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(building.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(5 * 4, court.Length);
        double sourceCourtGlazing = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(5 * sourceCourtGlazing, court.Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingPerOrientationCountsTheCourtFacades()
    {
        // The court façade of the south wing faces north: north glazing is that of the 30 m outer and the 10 m court façade.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();
        double ratio = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.WindowToWallRatio;

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees);

        Assert.All(totals.GlazingByOrientation.Values, g => Assert.Equal(ratio * (30.0 + 10.0) * 3.0, g, 9));
        Assert.Equal(ratio * 160.0 * 3.0, totals.Glazing, 9);
        Assert.Equal(160.0 * 3.0, totals.ExteriorWallArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.FloorArea, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AFloorThatLosesTheCourtGlazingFailsValidation()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();
        IFloor detailed = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;
        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(detailed.Surfaces);
        var floor = new Floor(
            plan,
            detailed.Zones,
            detailed.Surfaces.Select(s => s is WallSurface w && court.Contains(w) ? w.WithWindows(Array.Empty<Window>()) : s),
            detailed.Mapping,
            detailed.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);

        Assert.False(report.Passed);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "Glazing", "Glazing.North", "Glazing.East", "Glazing.South", "Glazing.West" },
            report.Checks.Where(c => !c.Passed).Select(c => c.Check));
    }

    private static FloorEntry[] Entries(IFloor floor) => new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) };

    private static double Overlap(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => new PolygonOps(Pipeline.Tolerances).IntersectionArea(a, b);

    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        return placement == 0 ? RingPlan.Generate() : RingPlan.Placed(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    }

    /// <summary>The outdoor walls on the court's edges, found in the plan frame before the placement.</summary>
    private static WallSurface[] CourtWalls(IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces, int placement = 0)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        var back = new PlanMotion(-p[0], 0.0, 0.0);
        Point2 Unplace(Point2 q) => back.Apply(new Point2(q.X - p[1], q.Y - p[2]));
        bool OnCourt(Point2 q) => Unplace(q) is var u && u.X is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6 && u.Y is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6;
        return surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w.Start) && OnCourt(w.End)).ToArray();
    }

    private static void AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IFloor floor, int outdoorWalls, int placement)
    {
        WallSurface[] outdoor = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(outdoorWalls, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
    }

    private static void AssertFloorValidates(IFloor floor)
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Footprints with a hole through the pipeline (S8.3) on <see cref="RingPlan"/>: a 30 m × 30 m footprint of four 10 m deep dwelling wings around a
/// 10 m × 10 m court. The court is outside the footprint; its edges are outdoor walls facing into it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RingFootprintTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="Lod.Core.Plans.PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 }, new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacementIndices() => Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length).Select(i => new object[] { i });

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator, placement };

    [Fact]
    public void TheRingPlanHasOutdoorCourtWallsWithWindows()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(plan.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, court.Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void NoSimplificationKeepsTheCourtWalls(int placement)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(placement);

        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
        AssertFloorValidates(floor);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheWingsIntoOneZoneAroundTheCourt(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone ring = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(ring.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(800.0, ring.FloorArea), $"Area {ring.FloorArea}");
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorKeepsTheCourtAsAHole(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new SingleZonePerFloor(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Zone floor = Assert.Single(result.Value.Zones);
        Assert.Single(floor.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, 8, placement);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacadesAroundARingCore()
    {
        // Bisector trapezoids on the outer ring and on the court's ring (ADR-008); the court's façades get their own perimeter zones, named by the
        // direction they face, and the core is what is left between them: a ring around the court.
        const double d = PerimeterDepth;
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, d).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "P-North", "P-East", "P-South", "P-West", "P-Court-North", "P-Court-East", "P-Court-South", "P-Court-West", "CORE" },
            floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal("Perimeter Court North", floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "P-Court-North").Name);
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-") && !z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((30.0 + 30.0 - (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), z => Assert.Equal((10.0 + 10.0 + (2 * d)) * d / 2.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(30.0 - (2 * d), 2) - Math.Pow(10.0 + (2 * d), 2), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.Single(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, floor.Zones.Sum(z => z.FloorArea), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CourtPerimeterZonesHoldTheCourtFacadesAndTheCoreHasNone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("P-Court-North", OrientationBin.North), ("P-Court-East", OrientationBin.East), ("P-Court-South", OrientationBin.South), ("P-Court-West", OrientationBin.West) },
            court.Select(w => (w.Zone.Value, Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth))));
        Assert.All(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")), w => Assert.Contains(w, court));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(floor, 8, 0);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacementIndices))]
    public void PerimeterCoreOfARingValidates(int placement)
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(Plan(placement));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(9, result.Value.Zones.Count);
        AssertFloorValidates(result.Value);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(8.0, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(9.14, 10.0)]
    [InlineData(4.0, 30.0)]
    public void WingsNotWiderThanTwiceTheDepthAreTooNarrow(double wing, double court)
    {
        // D-087 applies to every wing between the outer and the court façades: the outer and court perimeter bands would overlap.
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(wing, court));

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.EndsWith("every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth (9.14 m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACourtWhoseOffsetLeavesTheOuterOffsetIsTooNarrow()
    {
        // An off-centre court with a 2 m west wing: the offset court crosses the offset outer ring on the west, although the core polygon built from
        // the offset rings still has a positive area; clipping the offset court from the offset outer ring reveals it.
        Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 30, 30).Outer, new[] { Polygon2.Rectangle(2, 10, 12, 20).Outer }).Value;
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(footprint, 3.0, 0.0, Array.Empty<Zone>(), Array.Empty<Surface>(), Provenance.Of("Test", Array.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>()));

        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(plan);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith("Perimeter depth 4.57 m leaves no valid core between the outer and the court façades: every wing", error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingJustWiderThanTwiceTheDepthIsZoned()
    {
        Result<IFloor> result = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(RingPlan.Generate(9.2, 10.0));

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.Equal(0.06 * 4 * (10.0 + (2 * 4.57) + 0.06), result.Value.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").FloorArea, 6);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryAggregatorBuildsAndValidatesARingBuilding(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        IFloor floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(Plan(placement)).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor));

        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Stack", 5)]
    [InlineData("StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", 3)]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", 5)]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", 5)]
    public void TheCourtHasNoGroundSlabAndNoRoof(string aggregator, int storeysModelled)
    {
        // The court is outside the footprint: ground and roof are the footprint's 800 m², not the outer rectangle's 900 m².
        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value)).Value;

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces.ToArray(), building.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.GroundArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.RoofArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 6);
        Assert.All(building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary != BoundaryCondition.Interzone), h => Assert.Equal(0.0, Overlap(h.Polygon, Polygon2.Rectangle(10, 10, 20, 20)), 9));
        Assert.Equal(storeysModelled, building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts).Select(p => p.Elevation).Distinct().Count());
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("SingleZonePerFloorType", "E0,E1,E2")]
    [InlineData("SingleZoneBuilding", "BUILDING")]
    public void SingleZoneMethodsRebuildTheCourtWallsOfEveryStoreyWithCentredWindows(string aggregator, string zones)
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(RingPlan.Generate()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Entries(floor)).Value;

        Assert.Equal(zones, string.Join(",", building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.All(building.Zones.SelectMany(z => z.Parts), p => Assert.Single(p.Footprint.Holes));
        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(building.Surfaces);
        Assert.Equal(5 * 4, court.Length);
        double sourceCourtGlazing = CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(5 * sourceCourtGlazing, court.Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GlazingPerOrientationCountsTheCourtFacades()
    {
        // The court façade of the south wing faces north: north glazing is that of the 30 m outer and the 10 m court façade.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();
        double ratio = ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit.WindowToWallRatio;

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(plan.Zones, plan.Surfaces, plan.OrientationDegrees);

        Assert.All(totals.GlazingByOrientation.Values, g => Assert.Equal(ratio * (30.0 + 10.0) * 3.0, g, 9));
        Assert.Equal(ratio * 160.0 * 3.0, totals.Glazing, 9);
        Assert.Equal(160.0 * 3.0, totals.ExteriorWallArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(800.0, totals.FloorArea, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AFloorThatLosesTheCourtGlazingFailsValidation()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = RingPlan.Generate();
        IFloor detailed = new NoSimplification().Simplify(plan).Value;
        WallSurface[] court = CourtWalls(detailed.Surfaces);
        var floor = new Floor(
            plan,
            detailed.Zones,
            detailed.Surfaces.Select(s => s is WallSurface w && court.Contains(w) ? w.WithWindows(Array.Empty<Window>()) : s),
            detailed.Mapping,
            detailed.Provenance);

        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);

        Assert.False(report.Passed);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "Glazing", "Glazing.North", "Glazing.East", "Glazing.South", "Glazing.West" },
            report.Checks.Where(c => !c.Passed).Select(c => c.Check));
    }

    private static FloorEntry[] Entries(IFloor floor) => new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) };

    private static double Overlap(Polygon2 a, Polygon2 b) => new PolygonOps(Pipeline.Tolerances).IntersectionArea(a, b);

    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        return placement == 0 ? RingPlan.Generate() : RingPlan.Placed(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    }

    /// <summary>The outdoor walls on the court's edges, found in the plan frame before the placement.</summary>
    private static WallSurface[] CourtWalls(IEnumerable<Surface> surfaces, int placement = 0)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];
        var back = new PlanMotion(-p[0], 0.0, 0.0);
        Point2 Unplace(Point2 q) => back.Apply(new Point2(q.X - p[1], q.Y - p[2]));
        bool OnCourt(Point2 q) => Unplace(q) is var u && u.X is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6 && u.Y is > 10 - 1e-6 and < 20 + 1e-6;
        return surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w.Start) && OnCourt(w.End)).ToArray();
    }

    private static void AssertOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(IFloor floor, int outdoorWalls, int placement)
    {
        WallSurface[] outdoor = floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(outdoorWalls, outdoor.Length);
        Assert.All(outdoor, w => Assert.Equal((w.Length - Assert.Single(w.Windows).Width) / 2.0, w.Windows[0].Offset, 9));
        Assert.Equal(4, CourtWalls(floor.Surfaces, placement).Length);
    }

    private static void AssertFloorValidates(IFloor floor)
    {
        ValidationReport report = new FloorValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(floor);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.EnclosedCourt;

public sealed class EnclosedCourtGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly EnclosedCourtPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "S-ST1", "S-ST2", "S-ST3", "S-U1", "S-U2", "S-U3", "S-U4", "S-U5", "S-U6",
        "N-ST1", "N-ST2", "N-ST3", "N-U1", "N-U2", "N-U3", "N-U4", "N-U5", "N-U6",
        "W-ST1", "W-U1", "W-U2", "E-ST1", "E-U1", "E-U2",
    };

    private readonly EnclosedCourtGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new EnclosedCourtParameters(CourtWidth: 24.0, CourtDepth: 24.0, Depth: 12.0, UnitWidth: 7.0, StairWidth: 3.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            EnclosedCourtParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsFourStairBayWingsAroundACourt()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal((48.0 * 48.0) - (24.0 * 24.0), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "South Stair 2", "North Unit 3", "West Unit 2", "East Stair 1" }, plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value is "S-ST2" or "N-U3" or "W-U2" or "E-ST1").Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair), z => Assert.Equal(36.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("S-U") || z.Id.Value.StartsWith("N-U")), z => Assert.Equal(6.5 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.StartsWith("W-U") || z.Id.Value.StartsWith("E-U")), z => Assert.Equal(10.5 * 12.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(22.5, 0.0), new Point2(25.5, 0.0), new Point2(25.5, 12.0), new Point2(22.5, 12.0) }, Outer(plan, "S-ST2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(16.0, 36.0), new Point2(22.5, 36.0), new Point2(22.5, 48.0), new Point2(16.0, 48.0) }, Outer(plan, "N-U3"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0.0, 22.5), new Point2(12.0, 22.5), new Point2(12.0, 25.5), new Point2(0.0, 25.5) }, Outer(plan, "W-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(36.0, 25.5), new Point2(48.0, 25.5), new Point2(48.0, 36.0), new Point2(36.0, 36.0) }, Outer(plan, "E-U2"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheFootprintIsARectangleWithTheCourtAsItsOneHole()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(48, 0), new Point2(48, 48), new Point2(0, 48) }, plan.Footprint.Outer);
        IReadOnlyList<Point2> court = Assert.Single(plan.Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { new Point2(12, 12), new Point2(12, 36), new Point2(36, 36), new Point2(36, 12) }, court);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default).IntersectionArea(plan.Footprint, Polygon2.Rectangle(12, 12, 36, 36)), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0)]
    [InlineData(6.0, 40.0)]
    [InlineData(50.0, 4.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(double courtWidth, double courtDepth)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default with { CourtWidth = courtWidth, CourtDepth = courtDepth });

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Single(plan.Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S", "N", "W", "E" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value.Split('-')[0]).Distinct());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DwellingsLieOnEverySideOfTheClosedCourt()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default);
        WallSurface[] court = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && new[] { w.Start, w.End }.All(p => p.X is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0 && p.Y is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0))
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S-U2", "S-U3", "S-ST2", "S-U4", "S-U5" }, Facing(court, OrientationBin.North).OrderBy(w => w.End.X).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "N-U2", "N-U3", "N-ST2", "N-U4", "N-U5" }, Facing(court, OrientationBin.South).OrderBy(w => w.End.X).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "W-U1", "W-ST1", "W-U2" }, Facing(court, OrientationBin.East).OrderBy(w => w.Start.Y).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E-U1", "E-ST1", "E-U2" }, Facing(court, OrientationBin.West).OrderBy(w => w.End.Y).Select(w => w.Zone.Value));
        Assert.All(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, b => Assert.Equal(24.0, Facing(court, b).Sum(w => w.Length), 9));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryDwellingAdjoinsAStair()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default);

        Assert.All(
            plan.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit),
            z => Assert.Contains(PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, z.Id.Value), n => plan.Zones.Single(s => s.Id.Value == n).SpaceType == SpaceType.Stair));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S-ST1", "S-U1", "S-U2", "W-ST1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "W-U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E-ST1", "N-ST3", "N-U5", "N-U6" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "E-U2"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "S-U1", "S-U2", "W-U1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "S-ST1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "W-U1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "S-U3"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(44, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.Windows.Count));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, -24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtDepth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 0.0, 7.0, 3.0, "Depth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 12.0, double.NaN, 3.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(24.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, -3.0, "StairWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, "CourtWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double courtWidth, double courtDepth, double depth, double unitWidth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new EnclosedCourtParameters(courtWidth, courtDepth, depth, unitWidth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StairsThatLeaveNoRoomForDwellingsAreAnError()
    {
        // 24 m west and east wings with 30 m stairs: one bay needs more than the wing's length; the 48 m south and north wings (round(48 / 31) = 2
        // bays, 60 m of stairs) do not fit either.
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default with { UnitWidth = 0.5, StairWidth = 30.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 48 m south and north wing: its 2 stair bays need 60 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.",
                "StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 24 m west and east wing: its one stair bay needs 30 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.",
            },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Message));
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, d.Code));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new EnclosedCourtParameters(0.0, 0.0, -12.0, 0.0, 0.0, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(5, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default, new EnclosedCourtPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(EnclosedCourtGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "CourtDepth=24", "CourtWidth=24", "Depth=12", "FloorHeight=3", "OrientationDegrees=0", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=3", "UnitWidth=7" },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default)), "enclosed-court-canonical");
    }

    private static WallSurface[] Facing(WallSurface[] walls, OrientationBin bin) => walls.Where(w => Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == bin).ToArray();

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Generators/StairBayBar/StairBayLayout.cs` (full file)**

Only the class summary changes; `Bays`, `Wing`, `Fit`, and `FitErrors` are unchanged, and so are the S8.1 and S8.2 plans and snapshots.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

/// <summary>
/// The stair bay of ADR-006: dwelling | stair | dwelling side by side along a bar, each through the full depth, the stair serving the two dwellings
/// beside it. Shared by <see cref="StairBayBarGenerator"/> (HDHM-TYP-001), the stair pair (HDHM-TYP-008), and the wings of the winged band
/// (HDHM-TYP-003), the open court (HDHM-TYP-004), and the enclosed court (HDHM-TYP-005), so the bay geometry exists once.
/// </summary>
internal static class StairBayLayout
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays west to east from the origin. Zones: <c>ST1…STn</c> (stairs, west to east), then <c>U1…U2n</c>
    /// (dwellings, west to east); bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Bar depth, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the bar, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The footprint and the zones.</returns>
    public static PlanLayout Bays(int bays, double unitWidth, double depth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double length = bays * ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth);
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, length, depth), Wing(string.Empty, string.Empty, 0.0, 0.0, length, depth, alongY: false, bays, unitWidth, stairWidth));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The stair bays that fill a wing of length <paramref name="length"/> exactly (ADR-006): <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays, rounded
    /// half away from zero, with the dwelling width adjusted to <c>(L − n·S) / (2n)</c>, which is not positive when the stairs leave no room.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m; positive.</param>
    /// <returns>The bay count and the adjusted dwelling width.</returns>
    public static (int Bays, double UnitWidth) Fit(double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        double bays = Math.Max(1.0, Math.Round(length / ((2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth), MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));
        int count = bays >= int.MaxValue ? int.MaxValue : (int)bays;
        return (count, (length - (count * stairWidth)) / (2.0 * count));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The error for a wing whose fitted stair bays leave no dwelling width (<see cref="Fit"/>), or none; the message names the wing, such as
    /// <c>each 17 m wing</c>, and the stairs it would need.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="wing">The wing as named in the message.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length, m.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Target dwelling width, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width, m.</param>
    /// <returns>Zero or one <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter"/> error.</returns>
    public static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> FitErrors(string wing, double length, double unitWidth, double stairWidth)
    {
        (int bays, double width) = Fit(length, unitWidth, stairWidth);
        if (width > 0)
        {
            yield break;
        }

        string need = bays == 1
            ? $"its one stair bay needs {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m of stairs"
            : $"its {bays} stair bays need {Provenance.Format(bays * stairWidth)} m of stairs";
        yield return Diagnostic.Error(
            DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
            $"{nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} {Provenance.Format(stairWidth)} m leaves no dwelling width in {wing}: {need}; "
            + $"reduce {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.StairWidth)} or {nameof(StairBayBarParameters.UnitWidth)}.");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lays out <paramref name="bays"/> stair bays along a wing that starts at (<paramref name="x0"/>, <paramref name="y0"/>) and runs along +x, or
    /// along +y when <paramref name="alongY"/>; the last bay ends exactly at the wing's length. Zones: <c>{prefix}ST1…</c>, then <c>{prefix}U1…</c>,
    /// numbered along the wing's axis; bay <c>i</c> is <c>U(2i−1)</c>, <c>STi</c>, <c>U(2i)</c>.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="prefix">Zone ID prefix, such as <c>B-</c>.</param>
    /// <param name="namePrefix">Display name prefix, such as <c>Band </c>.</param>
    /// <param name="x0">Wing start, plan x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="y0">Wing start, plan y, m.</param>
    /// <param name="length">Wing length along its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="depth">Wing depth across its axis, m.</param>
    /// <param name="alongY">Whether the wing runs along +y instead of +x.</param>
    /// <param name="bays">Number of bays, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="unitWidth">Dwelling width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <param name="stairWidth">Stair width along the wing, m.</param>
    /// <returns>The stairs, then the dwellings.</returns>
    public static IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Wing(
        string prefix,
        string namePrefix,
        double x0,
        double y0,
        double length,
        double depth,
        bool alongY,
        int bays,
        double unitWidth,
        double stairWidth)
    {
        double bayWidth = (2.0 * unitWidth) + stairWidth;
        double start = alongY ? y0 : x0;
        Polygon2 Piece(double from, double to) => alongY ? Polygon2.Rectangle(x0, from, x0 + depth, to) : Polygon2.Rectangle(from, y0, to, y0 + depth);
        var stairs = new List<PlannedZone>();
        var units = new List<PlannedZone>();
        for (int i = 0; i < bays; i++)
        {
            double west = start + (i * bayWidth);
            double stairWest = west + unitWidth;
            double stairEast = stairWest + stairWidth;
            double east = i == bays - 1 ? start + length : west + bayWidth;
            stairs.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}ST{i + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Stair {i + 1}", SpaceType.Stair, Piece(stairWest, stairEast)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 1}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(west, stairWest)));
            units.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"{prefix}U{(2 * i) + 2}"), $"{namePrefix}Unit {(2 * i) + 2}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Piece(stairEast, east)));
        }

        stairs.AddRange(units);
        return stairs;
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/EnclosedCourt/EnclosedCourtGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;

namespace Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;

/// <summary>Parameters of the enclosed courtyard block (HDHM-TYP-005, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="CourtWidth">Width of the court between the west and east wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="CourtDepth">Depth of the court between the south and north wings, which is also the west and east wings' length, m.</param>
/// <param name="Depth">Depth of every wing, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the wings, m; adjusted so the stair bays fill each wing exactly.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of each stair along the wings, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record EnclosedCourtParameters(
    double CourtWidth,
    double CourtDepth,
    double Depth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: a 24 m × 24 m court, 12 m deep wings, 7 m dwellings and 3 m stairs, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static EnclosedCourtParameters Default { get; } = new(24.0, 24.0, 12.0, 7.0, 3.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the enclosed courtyard block, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stairs; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record EnclosedCourtPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Enclosed courtyard residential block (HDHM-TYP-005, ADR-006): four wings of stair bays around one court. Dwellings lie on every edge of the court
/// (R001), and the court is closed on all four sides (R002): the footprint is the outer rectangle with the court as its one hole, so the court is
/// outside the building and its façades are outdoor walls facing into it. The south and north wings run the full width and hold the corners; the
/// west and east wings run between them. Each wing holds <c>n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))</c> bays with the dwelling width adjusted to fill its
/// length <c>L</c>. One zone per dwelling unit (D-009).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// With <c>D</c> the depth, <c>w</c> the court width, and <c>c</c> the court depth: south wing <c>[0, 2D + w] × [0, D]</c>, north wing
/// <c>[0, 2D + w] × [D + c, 2D + c]</c>, west wing <c>[0, D] × [D, D + c]</c>, east wing <c>[D + w, 2D + w] × [D, D + c]</c>, court
/// <c>[D, D + w] × [D, D + c]</c>. Zone IDs: <c>S-ST1…</c>, <c>S-U1…</c> and <c>N-…</c> (west to east), then <c>W-…</c> and <c>E-…</c> (south to
/// north); display names <c>South Unit 3</c>, <c>West Stair 1</c>. The generator does not impose the precondition of <i>Perimeter Core</i> (D-087):
/// its default 12 m wings are wider than twice the 4.57 m default perimeter depth.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class EnclosedCourtGenerator : PlanGenerator<EnclosedCourtParameters, EnclosedCourtPresets>
{
    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public EnclosedCourtGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(EnclosedCourtGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(EnclosedCourtParameters parameters)
    {
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(EnclosedCourtParameters.CourtWidth), parameters.CourtWidth),
            (nameof(EnclosedCourtParameters.CourtDepth), parameters.CourtDepth),
            (nameof(EnclosedCourtParameters.Depth), parameters.Depth),
            (nameof(EnclosedCourtParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(EnclosedCourtParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        var errors = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (errors.Count == 0)
        {
            double width = Width(parameters);
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(width)} m south and north wing", width, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
            errors.AddRange(StairBayLayout.FitErrors($"each {Provenance.Format(parameters.CourtDepth)} m west and east wing", parameters.CourtDepth, parameters.UnitWidth, parameters.StairWidth));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(EnclosedCourtParameters p)
    {
        double d = p.Depth;
        double width = Width(p);
        double north = d + p.CourtDepth;
        double east = d + p.CourtWidth;
        (int longBays, double longUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(width, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        (int sideBays, double sideUnit) = StairBayLayout.Fit(p.CourtDepth, p.UnitWidth, p.StairWidth);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>();
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("S-", "South ", 0.0, 0.0, width, d, alongY: false, longBays, longUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("N-", "North ", 0.0, north, width, d, alongY: false, longBays, longUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("W-", "West ", 0.0, d, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        zones.AddRange(StairBayLayout.Wing("E-", "East ", east, d, p.CourtDepth, d, alongY: true, sideBays, sideUnit, p.StairWidth));
        Point2[] outer = { new(0.0, 0.0), new(width, 0.0), new(width, north + d), new(0.0, north + d) };
        Point2[] court = { new(d, d), new(d, north), new(east, north), new(east, d) };
        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Create(outer, new[] { court }).Value, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(EnclosedCourtParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CourtDepth), Provenance.Format(p.CourtDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Depth), Provenance.Format(p.Depth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    private static double Width(EnclosedCourtParameters p) => p.Depth + p.CourtWidth + p.Depth;
}
```

### Task 8: The enclosed court with every simplifier and aggregator

**Files:**
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/EnclosedCourtTypologyTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EnclosedCourtGenerator` and `EnclosedCourtParameters.Default` (Task 7); test support `Pipeline.Simplifier`, `Pipeline.Aggregator`, `Pipeline.Tolerances`; `PlanTransform.Apply(IGeneratedPlan plan, double rotationDegrees, double translationX, double translationY) : Result<IGeneratedPlan>` (S4.2); `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double)` (Task 3), `SingleZoneBuilding(ToleranceSettings)`, `FloorEntry(IFloor, int)`, `BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings).Validate`.
- Produces: the S8 test of the roadmap for the family with a hole: four simplifiers × four aggregators (floors 1, 3, 1) build and validate without warnings, on the canonical plan and on the plan rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m; *Semantic Merge* gives four L-shaped dwelling zones of 3 × 78 + 126 = 360 m², one around each corner of the court, and the eight stairs, none with a hole; *Perimeter Core* gives the eight perimeter zones and a ring core of 38.86² − 33.14² m², the court zones holding the court glazing; 9 m wings fail *Perimeter Core* with `PlanTooNarrow`; *Single Zone Building* keeps the court as a hole in every part, rebuilds the 20 court walls with one window each, and has 1728 m² of ground. No production code changes.

This task has no red phase: it checks that the simplifiers and aggregators, with Tasks 1 and 3, accept the new family. If any case fails, stop and report: a further pipeline change needs its own decision and test-first task.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/EnclosedCourtTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

52 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `SemanticMergeJoinsTheDwellingsAroundEachCornerOfTheCourt`, `PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacades`, `WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrow`, and `SingleZoneBuildingRebuildsTheCourtWallsOnEveryStorey` (1 each).

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>The S8.3 family with a hole (ADR-006): the enclosed courtyard block through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed.</summary>
public sealed class EnclosedCourtTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeJoinsTheDwellingsAroundEachCornerOfTheCourt()
    {
        // The stairs of the west and east wings and the middle stairs of the south and north wings separate the dwellings into four L-shaped groups,
        // one around each corner of the court; stairs never adjoin each other and stay apart.
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            "DwellingUnit-1,DwellingUnit-2,DwellingUnit-3,DwellingUnit-4,Stair-1,Stair-2,Stair-3,Stair-4,Stair-5,Stair-6,Stair-7,Stair-8",
            string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal((3 * 78.0) + 126.0, z.FloorArea, 6));
        Assert.All(floor.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreZonesTheOuterAndTheCourtFacades()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            "P-North,P-East,P-South,P-West,P-Court-North,P-Court-East,P-Court-South,P-Court-West,CORE",
            string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Polygon2 core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE").Parts[0].Footprint;
        Assert.Single(core.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(48.0 - (2 * 4.57), 2) - Math.Pow(24.0 + (2 * 4.57), 2), core.Area, 6);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "CORE" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        double courtGlazing = Plan().Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w)).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea);
        Assert.Equal(courtGlazing, floor.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value.StartsWith("P-Court")).Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WingsNarrowerThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrow()
    {
        // D-087 applies to the wings between the outer and the court façades: 9 m wings with the 4.57 m default depth leave no core.
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new EnclosedCourtGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default with { Depth = 9.0 }, Presets()).Value;

        Result<IFloor> floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(plan);

        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, Assert.Single(floor.Diagnostics).Code);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingRebuildsTheCourtWallsOnEveryStorey()
    {
        IFloor floor = new NoSimplification().Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(Pipeline.Tolerances).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) }).Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.All(zone.Parts, p => Assert.Single(p.Footprint.Holes));
        WallSurface[] court = building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && OnCourt(w)).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(5 * 4, court.Length);
        Assert.All(court, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(1728.0, building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Ground).Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
    }

    private static bool OnCourt(WallSurface wall) => new[] { wall.Start, wall.End }.All(p => p.X is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0 && p.Y is >= 12.0 and <= 36.0);

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static EnclosedCourtPresets Presets() => new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan() => new EnclosedCourtGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(EnclosedCourtParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

Make these replacements, in order; each "Replace" text occurs exactly once in the file when it is applied. The sentence of *Pipeline consequences of S8.2* corrected on `main` by `34deeaa` ("found three faults on rotated plans; the second …") is not touched.

1. Replace

```markdown
### Families implemented in S8.2 and specified for S8.3
```

   with

```markdown
### Families implemented in S8.2 and S8.3
```

2. Replace

```markdown
Records, defaults, and zoning are fixed here; the layouts are implemented, and may be refined, in their sub-stage, which records any change here. S8.2 implemented 007, 003, and 004 as specified, without changing a record or a default; the details it settled are recorded below each family. Each wing below is a bar of stair bays: `n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))` bays, rounded half away from zero, with the unit width adjusted to `(L − n·S) / (2n)` so the bays fill the wing's length `L` exactly. Validation additionally requires that adjusted width to be positive: stairs that leave no room for dwellings are `InvalidParameter`, for example `StairWidth 10 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m wing: its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.` Within a wing, zones are listed stairs first, then dwellings, numbered along the wing's axis, and each wing's last bay ends exactly at the wing's end.
```

Each wing below is a bar of stair bays: `n = max(1, round(L / (2U + S)))` bays, rounded half away from zero, with the unit width adjusted to `(L − n·S) / (2n)` so the bays fill the wing's length `L` exactly. Validation additionally requires that adjusted width to be positive: stairs that leave no room for dwellings are `InvalidParameter`, for example `StairWidth 10 m leaves no dwelling width in each 17 m wing: its 2 stair bays need 20 m of stairs; reduce StairWidth or UnitWidth.` Within a wing, zones are listed stairs first, then dwellings, numbered along the wing's axis, and each wing's last bay ends exactly at the wing's end.

The footprint's hole is the court ring `(D, D)`, `(D, D + c)`, `(D + w, D + c)`, `(D + w, D)`, stored clockwise. Validation also fits the stair bays to each wing as for the open court (`StairWidth 30 m leaves no dwelling width in each 48 m south and north wing: …`). The generator does not impose the *Perimeter Core* precondition of D-087, which belongs to that simplifier: the default 12 m wings are wider than twice the 4.57 m default perimeter depth, and 9 m wings fail *Perimeter Core* with `PlanTooNarrow`. *Semantic Merge* gives four L-shaped dwelling zones, one around each corner of the court (the side wings' stairs and the middle stairs of the long wings separate them), and the eight stairs; no merged zone has a hole.

Its precondition, that every wing or other part of the footprint be wider than twice the perimeter depth, is stated in its `PlanTooNarrow` message and in [ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md); with the defaults (12 m deep wings and plates of 22 m, 4.57 m depth) every family passes.
