## Exit criteria

- The surface builder requires every footprint edge, outer ring and holes, to be covered by outdoor zone edges over its full length (`UnmatchedEdge`), tested by `LayoutSurfaceBuilderTests` with a test that failed on the code before it; ADR-002 describes the rule and footprints with holes.
- *Perimeter Core* zones footprints with holes: every ring offset by the bisector rule, the court zones `P-Court-<Bin>` after the outer zones, a ring core, and `PlanTooNarrow` for a wing between the outer and a court façade that is not wider than twice the depth (D-087); tested by `RingFootprintTests` with tests that failed on the code before it and by the crossing-offset case; ADR-008 describes it. `FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet` is removed, and no other test.
- *No Simplification*, *Semantic Merge*, *Single Zone per Floor*, the four floor aggregators, and validation handle the ring footprint, canonical and at two rotated and moved placements, with the court as a hole and no ground slab or roof under it (`RingFootprintTests`, 76 cases); ADR-013 records it.
- `EnclosedCourtGenerator` (005, four wings of stair bays around a court closed on all four sides, the court the footprint's one hole) implements ADR-006 with its defaults, zone IDs, and validation; it has tiling, court façade, stair, window, invalid-parameter, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests.
- The enclosed court validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plan and at two rotated and moved placements (`EnclosedCourtTypologyTests`, 52 cases).
- Snapshots: the fifteen existing snapshots byte-identical; one new canonical snapshot.
- One new component in *2 Generate* with the GUID of this plan, its ADR-006 defaults, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last, and an icon (36 icons); no existing component, input, or GUID changed.
- `RhinoGeometry` follows D-091 for every plan generator: zone solids outward-oriented, walls and windows facing their wall's outward side, floors down, ceilings up; `convert2bem.md` states it; the smoke test observed it.
- ADR-006 records 005 as implemented and the pipeline consequences of S8.3; `pipeline.md`, `validation.md`, and the precedent page describe S8.3; the S8.3 smoke-test checklist is in the guide.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 158 core, 181 generators, 601 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- The S8.3 smoke test is recorded in the close-out commit, with the items left for a person named.
- Version 0.8.2, local tag `v0.8.2`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line and detailed-plans row, and the decision-log entry D-092 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- Nothing is pushed in this run (D-084); Task 18 is left for the owner.

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and documentation replacement was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on the S8.2 reference (`09c0ac3`), 14 commits ending at `754707f`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings. Local `main` is `87bd272`: S8.2 executed (`v0.8.1`; `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` identical to the S8.2 reference, `Directory.Build.props` at 0.8.1) plus the follow-up commits `f27f7e2` (the S8.2 plan's contact-sheet text), `34deeaa` (a corrected sentence in ADR-006's *Pipeline consequences of S8.2*), `1566f85` (D-090), and `87bd272` (D-091). Every file the reference touches is identical on `main` and in the S8.2 reference except ADR-006: Task 9's replacements are taken from the reference and checked against `main`'s ADR-006, none of them touches the sentence of `34deeaa`, and the result equals the reference's ADR-006 with `main`'s sentence. The reference's commit order is kept with three exceptions, each of which moves a commit across commits that touch none of its files, so every file content in this plan is the content of the reference commit it comes from: the test of a court offset crossing the outer offset (`338f543`, written after the component in the reference) is Task 6, with the other ring tests; the ADR commit (`24400ff`) is Task 9, at the end of Slice A, so that the rules of Tasks 1 and 3 and the ADRs that state them are merged together (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7; note 9); and the component commit (`58b5f6a`) is split into Task 11 (the component) and Task 12 (the icon and `make_icons.py`), as S8.1 and S8.2 did. The reference has no roadmap commit; the roadmap edits are the plan commit's and the close-out's. Every task was then replayed on a clone of `87bd272`, taking each file content and each replacement from this plan's text: the failing tests and messages of Tasks 1 and 3, the red errors of Task 7 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), its single failing snapshot test and the received snapshot (byte-identical to the expected content), the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 12 and the 36 hashes after it (all 36 PNGs byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied, and the result equals the reference, ADR-006 with `main`'s sentence and `program-presets.md` with the anchor of note 10), the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on a fresh clone of the replayed `v0.8.2` (158 core, 181 generators, 601 integration) are observed results. The replayed end state equals the reference apart from line endings, the version (the reference keeps 0.5.0), and the files the reference holds in their S8.2 form or not at all (the decision log, the roadmap, AGENTS.md, README.md, the plans, the `.gitignore` lines `main` gained in S8.1, ADR-006's sentence of `34deeaa`, and the anchor in `program-presets.md`).
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.3; this plan uses four slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `feature/footprints-with-holes` (which also holds the `fix(core)` commit of Task 1 and the ADRs of Task 9), `feature/grasshopper-enclosed-court`, `fix/grasshopper-orientation` (D-091, type `fix`, its own slice because it changes the output of every plan generator, not only the new one), `docs/architecture-enclosed-court`, and `chore/repo-s8.3-close-out`. Merges rebase onto local `main`. Like S8.1 and S8.2, this run ends without a push (D-084); Task 18 is written out for the owner, pushes `v0.8.0`, `v0.8.1`, and `v0.8.2` (any the remote lacks), and stays unticked. On the machine this plan was written on, the remote-tracking `origin/main` is at `274fff9` (S8.2 Task 4), recorded in the reflog as "update by push", although the S8.2 records say nothing was pushed in that run; local `main` contains it, so a push stays a fast-forward. Task 18, Step 1 checks this and the remote's tags before anything is pushed.
3. **The removed test.** `SimplifierTests.FootprintsWithHolesAreNotSupportedYet` (S3) asserted that *Perimeter Core* rejects a ring footprint with `NotSupported`, the placeholder that D-043 and ADR-008 announced "until S8 (courtyard plans)". Task 3 replaces that behaviour, as D-084 assigns it to S8.3, so the test is removed in the same commit; it is the only test S8.3 removes or changes. This does not weaken a check: the replacing tests require more (the zones, their areas, the court façades and glazing, validation at three placements), and the narrow rings it might have covered now fail with `PlanTooNarrow`, also tested. The constant `DiagnosticCodes.NotSupported` stays (removing a public constant is not needed and would touch an S3 file for nothing); no code emits it after Task 3, and `pipeline.md` and `validation.md` drop it from the simplifier preconditions (Task 14).
4. **`P-Court-<Bin>` naming.** The court façade zones are separate from the outer façade zones of the same orientation bin, and are named by the direction they face: the south wing's court façade faces north and belongs to `P-Court-North`. They are listed after the four outer zones and before `CORE`, with the S3 suffix rule (`-1`, `-2`) for several separate runs in one bin. The reasons are in ADR-008 (Task 9): a court façade faces a court, not the street, and its band never touches the outer band of the same bin (the core lies between them), so uniting them would always give two polygons numbered `P-North-1` and `P-North-2` in an order that says nothing about which is which. This is a zoning rule chosen by the controller (unsupervised run, D-084) and recorded in ADR-008 and in the completion entry D-092, without a decision-log entry of its own; the owner may revisit it at the reading of ADR-008. *Perimeter Core* remains a Z2 strategy; the court zones change no equivalence rule, since every zone keeps its areas, loads, and glazing by the existing rules.
5. **The boundary coverage rule (Task 1).** Edge matching splits zone edges at other zones' vertices, pairs reversed edges as interzone walls, and marks edges on the footprint boundary as outdoors; any other edge is an error. It cannot see a zone lying outside the footprint whose every edge is shared with another zone, such as a zone filling a court: the court's edges become interzone walls and nothing is left unmatched (`AZoneFillingTheCourtIsAnError` failed with `Assert.False() Failure` before the fix). The new check requires the outdoor segments on each edge of every footprint ring, outer ring and holes, to add up to the edge's length. The lengths are compared with `AreaEquals`, the relative tolerance with which façade coverage is compared (D-041); no tolerance is added, and the segment test is the S8.2 `IsOnSegment` on original positions, so rotated plans pass (`ARotatedRingKeepsItsCourtWalls`). Three of the four new tests pass before the fix: the surface builder has treated hole edges as plan boundary since S2; they document that behaviour. Every existing plan covers its footprint edges, so every snapshot is unchanged; the check can only reject layouts that were wrong.
6. **The orientation fix applies to every family and to released versions (D-091, Task 13).** D-091 was logged on `main` after the reference's first headless check found every *Convert2BEM* zone solid inward-oriented (negative volume) and every floor facing up, on every plan of `v0.8.1` and earlier, not only on courts: `PlanarBreps` gave each face the upward normal of its counter-clockwise outer ring, and the extrusion of an upward floor is an inward solid. The fix changes orientation only (faces built unreversed with the facing they need, the solid extruded from the downward floor, and the `Outward` and `Facing` guards), never geometry, and it changes the output of every plan generator and of the merged previews, so it is a slice of its own (type `fix`). Grasshopper code has no unit tests (RhinoCommon needs Rhino); the evidence is the second headless session on the reference's last commit (Task 16, item 7: positive volumes; walls, floors, ceilings, and windows facing out of their zones on the linear plan, the enclosed court, and a rotated *Single Zone Building*). The state before the fix is described by D-091 and was not re-observed for this plan. The *Convert2BEM* output layout and every GUID are unchanged; tools that read the Breps see only corrected orientation (D-064). The reference's `convert2bem.md` and `RhinoGeometry` remarks state the rule without citing D-091, which was logged after them; the plan keeps the reference text, and the roadmap edits, AGENTS.md, README.md, and D-092 cite it.
7. **The D-090 limit.** S8.3 does not change the small-zone limit of D-090. The reference build reported that the enclosed court with extreme parameters fails `SourceCoverage` on rotated plans after a merging simplifier in the same way as the open court: with Court Depth 4 m each side wing holds one bay of 0.5 m dwellings. This was not replayed. No tolerance is widened (AGENTS.md rule 12); every default plan validates at every tested placement (Task 8, Task 16).
8. **Tests that were not test-first, and why.** Tasks 2, 4, and 5 add 66 tests that passed at once in the reference and in the replay: *No Simplification*, *Semantic Merge* (tiles since S8.2), *Single Zone per Floor*, the floor aggregators (designation on the stack since S4.2, outlines as tiles since S8.2), and validation already handled a hole, so no code change was needed and no red phase is possible; the tests record that the brief's question "does this step need work for holes?" was answered by observation, and each task says to stop if a case fails. Task 6 adds one test that covers a branch of Task 3's code that no case of Task 3 reaches: a probe on the replayed end state (not committed) showed that the three precondition cases of Task 3 and the 9 m enclosed court are all rejected by the earlier check, with the message `… leaves no valid core for this footprint: …`, while the off-centre court of Task 6 gives `… between the outer and the court façades: …`. The reference wrote this test after the Grasshopper component, as a review follow-up. Task 8 is the combination matrix (no red phase, as in S8.1 and S8.2). Tasks 10 to 13 are Grasshopper code. The build-error red phase of Task 7 lists only declaration-level errors (four): the compiler stops before binding method bodies.
9. **ADRs in Slice A.** S8.2 put each ADR change in the task of its code; the reference of S8.3 wrote all ADR changes in one commit after the code. This plan keeps that commit whole but moves it into Slice A (Task 9), because the ADR texts cite the enclosed court of Task 7 and the matrix of Task 8 (ADR-008's numbers for the default court, ADR-013's "every combination … validates"), so they cannot land with Tasks 1 or 3; Slice A is merged only after Task 9, so `main` never holds the rules without their ADRs.
10. **A link the reference missed.** The reference renamed the heading `## Grasshopper components through S8.2` of `pipeline.md` to S8.3 but left the link in `docs/research/program-presets.md` at `#grasshopper-components-through-s82`, which would no longer resolve (the replay's link check found it). Task 14, Step 5 changes the anchor, as S8.2 did for the same link; it is the only documentation content of this plan not taken from the reference.
11. **ADR-006's corrected sentence.** `main`'s ADR-006 carries the wording of `34deeaa` ("found three faults on rotated plans; the second (`UnmatchedEdge` in *Semantic Merge*) also affected the linear plan and the gallery bar at some placements in released versions up to `v0.8.0` (D-088);"), which the reference lacks. Task 9 keeps it: no replacement contains it, and the replay compared the result with the reference plus that sentence.
12. **D-087 stays a simplifier precondition.** The generator validates only its own parameters (positive lengths, stair bays that leave a dwelling width); it does not reject wings narrower than twice a perimeter depth, which is a property of *Perimeter Core*'s depth input, not of the plan. The default 12 m wings pass with the 4.57 m default depth; 9 m wings fail *Perimeter Core* with `PlanTooNarrow` (Task 8 and the smoke test), and the other simplifiers accept them.
13. ***Semantic Merge* on the court.** Merging the default enclosed court gives four L-shaped dwelling zones of 360 m², one around each corner of the court, and the eight stairs; no merged zone has a hole, because the stairs of the west and east wings and the middle stairs of the south and north wings separate the dwellings. A merged zone with a hole around the court is tested on the ring footprint of Task 2, whose four wings are all dwellings.
14. **Icons.** The 35 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 12 (Pillow 12.3.0); the new one equals the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet description is from the replay's sheet.
15. **Smoke-test values.** The expected values of Task 16 were observed in two scripted headless Rhino 8.25 sessions (D-056) on the reference's last commit, which reported the informational version `0.5.0+754707f…`: registration and inputs, the default plan (bounding box, the footprint line of *Inspect*, *Boundaries*, *Windows*, *Zones*, and the zone counts of three simplifiers), the Breps with holes (faces, inner loops, validity, volumes, boxes) of three combinations, the 16 canonical and 32 rotated combinations (states and *Validate* Passed), the orientation checks, and three parameter scenarios. They were not re-observed on the replay. *Inspect* texts beyond the footprint line, the zone IDs, the *Validate* glazing values, *Convert2BEM* on most combinations, and the viewport were not observed.
16. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
17. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 to S8.2.
